# FlightRight Whisper-small ATC P1: evaluation and testing

Own LoRA fine-tune on openai/whisper-small; configured default when its local export exists. Local-path separator variants denote the same model.

[10 November continuation study](asr_small_atc_20261110.md): new offline candidate and mixed held-out results; original app model retained.

[Latest comparison](../evaluation.md) · [Every original source and cleanup status](source_inventory.json)

This is the single maintained history for this model/component. Records preserve the original bytes, including every numeric field, trial, seed, protocol, selection and caveat. Shared reports are included in each relevant ledger. No fresh inference is implied by consolidation.

## Recorded speech/extraction iterations

WER and accuracies are percentages. Different corpora and raw/numeral-normalized WER must not be ranked together. `NaN` means unavailable, never zero. Rejected number-rule results remain history only.

## LoRA training checkpoints

r=32, 12,976,128 trainable parameters; 7,330 ATCOSIM and 10,172 UWB clips, learning rate 0.0005, batch 8 with accumulation 2; validation uses 200 clips per corpus. Training-validation scores below differ from the exported CTranslate2 test decoder.

| Step | ATCOSIM val WER % | UWB val WER % | Mean val WER % |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 0 | 83.76 | 93.47 | 88.62 |
| 500 | 18.99 | 20.9 | 19.95 |
| 1000 | 19.22 | 18.8 | 19.01 |
| 1500 | 13.84 | 16.41 | 15.12 |
| 2000 | 13.95 | 13.42 | 13.68 |
| 2500 | 13.84 | 13.62 | 13.73 |
| 3000 | 12.91 | 12.87 | 12.89 |
| 3282 | 12.98 | 12.12 | 12.55 |

## Evaluation update 2026-11-10

This dated addition supplements the unchanged earlier history. Latest deployment statements below supersede earlier deployment status only.

## November 2026 continuation: current served model and complete experiment

**Current default: uniform_soup3, activated on 11 November at the user's explicit request.** This is the validation-selected combined model. Independent test results are mixed; activation does not turn the UWB regression into an improvement. Original P1 weights remain available as fallback. The prediction target, text normalizer, decoder and alert thresholds are unchanged.

The purpose was to improve speech-to-text using existing recorded speech and previously unused Indian-accent generated practice calls. Two independent continuations start from the original best P1 adapter, not from each other. A third candidate averages the full weights of the original and both continuations equally. Identical tensors stay bit-exact; tied weights remain tied. All three source weight hashes were verified unchanged before/after averaging (287 tensors preserved, 192 averaged). The average is one ordinary Whisper model, not an inference ensemble.

### How the data and comparisons were kept fair

Base: openai/whisper-small; original adapter: local voice cache runs/p1_main/best_adapter. Exact base/adapter identities, original manifest hashes and synthetic-manifest hashes are in the protocol evidence. The exact pre-change training/source files were preserved under the archived source_at_training paths; the source snapshot pins their SHA-256 values.

| Corpus | Split | Clips | Hours |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| atco2 | test | 871 | 0.987 |
| atcosim | test | 1712 | 2.045 |
| atcosim | train | 7330 | 7.765 |
| atcosim | val | 516 | 0.636 |
| svarah | test | 6656 | 9.614 |
| tts_indian | train | 3341 | 4.933 |
| tts_indian | val | 416 | 0.599 |
| uwb | test | 2814 | 2.428 |
| uwb | train | 10172 | 9.485 |
| uwb | val | 1116 | 1.048 |

ATCOSIM is human speech in simulation: train sm1/sm2/sm4/zf1/zf2, validation gm1/gf1, test sm3/zf3/gm2. UWB keeps published recording-disjoint test data, drops the overlapping recording, and chooses validation recordings by the existing SHA-1 modulo-10 rule. Speaker identities are unavailable for UWB. No ATCO2 audio is used for training; 559 mirror clips remain excluded. ATCO2 test has 762 primary clean clips and 109 clips contaminated for another published comparator, not for this model. Both subsets remain reported. Svarah is test-only ordinary Indian English, not aviation radio; accent and vocabulary are confounded.

Generated practice validation has 416 calls, separate aircraft identities and three held-out generated voices (Ravi, Riya, Vikas). Noise comes only from UWB training audio. This is not real Indian controller speech. Training has 3,341 generated calls. The audio audit checked 34,944 usable decoded waveforms: all distinct, no exact cross-split duplicates, valid bounds and durations. It does not exclude all possible near-duplicates or shared unknown speakers. All 22,891 training/validation targets fit: maximum 49 tokens, zero over the trainer limit.

### Every training trial and checkpoint

Both use seed 42, LoRA rank 32/alpha 64/dropout .05 on q/k/v/out and fc1/fc2 projections (12,976,128 trainable parameters), FP16 autocast, gradient checkpointing, AdamW weight decay .01, gradient clipping 1.0, batch 8 accumulated twice (effective batch 16), warmup 30, at most 300 optimizer updates, evaluation every 100 and patience 3. A new optimizer/schedule is used for each continuation; this is initialization from P1, not a resumed legacy optimizer. The starting checkpoint stays eligible. Checkpoint selection uses the mean of the two recorded-speech validation WERs; generated speech never chooses a checkpoint. Training monitoring uses a fixed seed-42 sample of 200 clips per corpus and greedy GPU decoding; these numbers are distinct from the full production-decoder validation/test scores.

| Trial | Stage | LR | Updates completed | Best update | Available train pool | Weighted epoch pool |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| continue_lr1e4 | p1 | 0.0001 | 300 | 300 | {"atcosim": 7330, "uwb": 10172} | {"atcosim": 7330, "uwb": 10172} |
| indian_mix_lr5e5 | p2 | 5e-05 | 300 | 100 | {"atcosim": 7330, "tts_indian": 3341, "uwb": 10172} | {"atcosim": 1832, "tts_indian": 3341, "uwb": 2543} |

P1 uses all recorded ATCOSIM/UWB data in the epoch pool. P2 uses all generated data and 25% of each recorded corpus per epoch, then stops after the stated 300 updates. Available pool size is not a claim that every item was consumed in this short continuation. P2 update 100 was retained: later generated-speech gains worsened recorded-speech validation.

### Validation selection, before final-test inference

Export each candidate to CTranslate2 int8_float16. On all 1,632 recorded validation clips (516 ATCOSIM and 1,116 UWB), use identical English transcribe, beam 5, temperature 0, no timestamps, no prior-text conditioning and no VAD (`fw1-beam5-v1`). Choose the lowest equal-corpus mean WER, allowing at most 0.5 percentage points regression per real corpus; the original stays eligible. Generated voices and final-test scores do not select the winner.

| Configuration | ATCOSIM validation WER % | UWB validation WER % | Outcome |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| baseline | 12.1 | 10.64 | unselected |
| continue_lr1e4 | 12.08 | 10.79 | unselected |
| indian_mix_lr5e5 | 12.67 | 10.99 | unselected |
| uniform_soup3 | 11.92 | 10.41 | selected |

The uniform average was a documented validation-only amendment after the real-only continuation failed to beat the original on full validation and P2 showed forgetting. The selection rule stayed fixed. The coordinator restarted with 100 P2 validation clips cached; training and exports were already complete, no progress was lost, and no selection/test report existed. Final choice frozen at 2026-11-10T10:08:20.785274+00:00. Only that winner received new full test inference.

### Final test: measurements from 10 November, activation on 11 November

WER counts substitutions, deletions and insertions per 100 reference words; lower is better, and it is not sentence accuracy. Do not combine corpora into one accuracy number. The selected model freshly transcribed 12,053 test clips. Original-model predictions were cached and re-scored using the same current normalizer/extractor, not newly inferred in full. Fresh original-model controls on 64 clips in each of four corpora (256 total, seed 42) matched cached normalized text and log-probability exactly. Timing includes cached and concurrent-load observations; it is not a new isolated latency benchmark.

| Corpus | Clips | Original WER % | Current WER % | Original numeral-normalized % | Current numeral-normalized % |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| atco2_clean | 762 | 28.29 | 27.95 | 28.29 | 27.95 |
| atco2_contaminated | 109 | 17.4 | 17.34 | 17.4 | 17.34 |
| atcosim_heldout_speakers | 1712 | 4.88 | 4.8 | 4.88 | 4.8 |
| uwb_heldout_recordings | 2814 | 15.11 | 15.33 | 15.11 | 15.33 |
| svarah_indian_english | 6656 | 33.17 | 31.46 | 28.9 | 27.22 |

Complete callsign + action + value instructions on ATCO2 clean: **20/31 (64.52%) -> 21/31 (67.74%)**. Plausible-tag subset: 19/24 -> 20/24. The small, imperfectly tagged instruction subset is not a certification or a measured end-to-end runtime alert gain. Callsign matching uses the same proxy list from annotated airport/frequency/day for both models, not a prompt to the recognizer. All action, value, callsign, extraction-agreement and per-first-language results remain verbatim in the test evidence. No accuracy is invented for unselected models' tests: not run.

Generated-voice development diagnostic, after selection froze: 416 held-out generated calls, same runtime decoder, original WER **24.62%**, selected **16.60%**. This does not measure real Indian controllers. No real Indian radio benchmark was available/used in this study.

### Uncertainty, rejected work and implementation checks

| Corpus | Clusters | Word-error change, percentage points | Paired cluster 95% interval |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| atco2_clean | 452 | -0.3406 | [-0.825, 0.1593] |
| atcosim | 3 | -0.0884 | [-0.1348, -0.0708] |
| uwb | 676 | 0.2171 | [-0.0797, 0.4924] |

Paired cluster percentile bootstrap: 2,000 resamples, seed 42, recording clusters for ATCO2/UWB and speaker clusters for ATCOSIM. Negative changes mean fewer errors. ATCOSIM has only three test speakers. No speaker-level Svarah interval is claimed because IDs are absent. The ATCO2/UWB intervals include zero; these small effects are uncertain. The historical test has been inspected in earlier work, so this is retrospective exploratory evidence, not a fresh blind benchmark.

Unselected/rejected work is retained: real-only continuation; accent continuation and its later worse checkpoints; four-worker speed probe (290 matching transcripts/confidences but no consistent speed gain); initial batch probe with a feature-cropping error; corrected batch probe with 14/128, 13/128 and 14/128 transcript differences. Neither speed variant was adopted. The batch findings describe our experimental code, not a library defect. Official scores always use the unchanged sequential production decoder.

Trainer repairs: final partial gradient groups are processed/scaled, exact data cursor and random/scaler state are saved, legacy non-exact resumes are refused, loading failures propagate, initial weights stay eligible, synthetic WER does not select real-radio checkpoints, and existing runs/exports are not silently overwritten. Export supports isolated output paths; runtime loading accepts an already converted complete folder. All 90 pre-activation Python tests passed, including nine focused training/selection/averaging checks. Today's activation check loaded the actual configured SpeechService and reproduced evaluated ATCOSIM/UWB transcripts.

```python
from server.app.config import ASR_MODEL_ID
from ml_pipeline.voice.transcribe import load_model, DECODING

```

Weights use UWB-ATCC CC BY-NC-SA 4.0 research data. Research and source references: [Whisper training guide](https://huggingface.co/blog/fine-tune-whisper), [Whisper-ATC](https://research.tudelft.nl/en/publications/whisper-atc-open-models-for-air-traffic-control-automatic-speech-/), [model soups](https://proceedings.mlr.press/v162/wortsman22a.html), [cluster resampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/1912.09508), [UWB source/license](https://huggingface.co/datasets/Jzuluaga/uwb_atcc). Learning rates, trial budget, 0.5-point guard, equal blend and cluster choices are our experimental design, not published guarantees. Additional sources were researched; no new corpus was downloaded.

All working reports, diagnostic sources, preserved training-source copies, logs and report-specific prediction data are retained losslessly below with SHA-256 and in source_inventory.json. The user requested removal of the separate dated reports after consolidation; no model/data cleanup is implied. Restore any individual historical input with `venv/Scripts/python.exe scripts/consolidate_evaluations.py --restore ORIGINAL_PATH`. Historical finalize.py is archived evidence, not the current publisher. The active study tools read the archive without recreating deleted reports. New experiments must use new run paths.

```json
      "content": "# Flight Right speech model \u2014 10 November 2026\r\n\r\nSelected version: **uniform_soup3**. Turns short English radio recordings into text.\r\n\r\nTwo additional training trials were compared with the original model and one averaged model. All four were compared on the same 1,632 validation recordings using the application's unchanged decoder. The choice was fixed before the selected model received new final-test inference.\r\n\r\nA fresh original-model check on 256 test recordings across all four collections matched the cached original transcripts and confidence values exactly.\r\n\r\nFinal-test results are mixed: some collections improve and another worsens. Every collection is reported separately below.\r\n\r\n| Recording collection | Clips | Previous word mistakes per 100 | Selected word mistakes per 100 |\r\n| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: |\r\n| Separate real-radio collection (ATCO2 clean) | 762 | 28.29 | 27.95 |\r\n| Simulation recordings, separate speakers (ATCOSIM) | 1712 | 4.88 | 4.8 |\r\n| Separate radio recordings (UWB) | 2814 | 15.11 | 15.33 |\r\n| Everyday Indian English (Svarah; not radio) | 6656 | 33.17 | 31.46 |\r\n\r\nComplete annotated instructions understood correctly: **20/31 \u2192 21/31**. This is a small sample, with known annotation limitations recorded in the evaluation ledger.\r\n\r\nWord mistakes count omitted, added or substituted words per 100 reference words. They do not measure the percentage of whole sentences correct.\r\n\r\nThese are historical evaluation recordings used in previous project work, not a new blind benchmark. Real Indian controller radio accuracy remains unmeasured: generated practice voices and everyday Indian English are different tasks. Speaker/recording resampling uncertainty is in uncertainty.json; the simulation test has only three speakers.\r\n\r\n## Separate generated-voice diagnostic\r\n\r\nOn 416 generated Indian-accent practice recordings, word error was 24.62% \u2192 16.6%. The voices and aircraft identities were kept out of training. This is a development diagnostic on generated audio, not a measurement of real Indian controller radio accuracy.\r\n\r\n## Files and use\r\n\r\nRuntime model folder: `C:\\Users\\singh\\OneDrive\\Desktop\\Archit\\KJ Somaiya\\LY Project\\Flight_Right\\checkpoints\\voice_20261110\\ct2\\local__uniform_soup3__int8_float16`\r\n\r\nFull-precision training/export folder: `C:\\Users\\singh\\OneDrive\\Desktop\\Archit\\KJ Somaiya\\LY Project\\Flight_Right\\checkpoints\\voice_20261110\\models\\uniform_soup3`\r\n\r\nThe running application's model configuration has not been changed by this study. The runtime folder was loaded directly and reproduced an evaluated validation transcript.\r\n\r\nFrom the project environment:\r\n\r\n```python\r\nfrom ml_pipeline.voice.transcribe import load_model, DECODING\r\nmodel = load_model(\"PATH_TO_RUNTIME_FOLDER\", device=\"auto\")\r\nsegments, _ = model.transcribe(\"recording.wav\", **DECODING)\r\nprint(\" \".join(segment.text.strip() for segment in segments))\r\n```\r\n\r\nRuntime format: CTranslate2 int8_float16; CPU loading uses int8. The full Whisper export is also retained for future training.\r\n\r\nTraining includes UWB-ATCC data under CC BY-NC-SA 4.0; this is a noncommercial research artifact. Source: https://huggingface.co/datasets/Jzuluaga/uwb_atcc\r\n\r\nRecorded conflict-detection early catches remain 270/284 (95.1%), not remeasured. This study did not change conflict models or alert thresholds.\r\n"

      "encoding": "utf-8",
      "content": "# Flight Right Whisper-small continuation study: 10 November 2026\r\n\r\nValidation-selected uniform weight average of the original and two continued-training models. An offline candidate; the application's original speech model remains configured.\r\n\r\n[Complete study](../experiments/voice_tuning_20261110.md) \u00b7 [Model card](../../ml_pipeline/reports/voice_tuning_20261110/MODEL_CARD.md)\r\n\r\n| Recording collection | Clips | Previous word mistakes per 100 | Selected word mistakes per 100 |\r\n| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: |\r\n| Separate real-radio collection (ATCO2 clean) | 762 | 28.29 | 27.95 |\r\n| Simulation recordings, separate speakers (ATCOSIM) | 1712 | 4.88 | 4.8 |\r\n| Separate radio recordings (UWB) | 2814 | 15.11 | 15.33 |\r\n| Everyday Indian English (Svarah; not radio) | 6656 | 33.17 | 31.46 |\r\n\r\nComplete instructions: 20/31 -> 21/31. These historical test data were used in previous work; results are exploratory. The synthetic-voice diagnostic is not real Indian radio accuracy.\r\n\r\nExact protocol and its pre-test amendment, every validation trial, all final test metrics, paired uncertainty estimates, software environment, model-file fingerprints and final source hashes remain under `ml_pipeline/reports/voice_tuning_20261110/`. New weights and prediction caches remain local under `checkpoints/voice_20261110/`. No historical evidence is removed or replaced.\r\n"

    {
      "path": "docs/experiments/voice_tuning_20261110.md",
      "sha256": "a04f26b42065ca32cda7ebbfb36d13077d013b8b1a2ab05a11216ae01d18692c",
      "bytes": 10210,
      "encoding": "utf-8",
      "content": "# Speech recognition tuning \u2014 10 November 2026\r\n\r\nThis study asks whether additional training improves the existing model that turns\r\npilot/controller recordings into written words. It does not change conflict alerts.\r\nThe recorded conflict-detection result remains 270/284 (95.1%); it is not remeasured here.\r\n\r\n## Rules fixed before comparing results\r\n\r\n- Keep the existing speaker/recording-based training, validation and test separation.\r\n  Validation recordings choose settings; test recordings measure the chosen result.\r\n- Try two separate continuations of the existing best adapter: real radio only at\r\n  learning rate 0.0001, and real radio mixed with Indian-accent generated practice calls\r\n  at 0.00005. Each gets up to 300 updates, a 30-update warmup, and checks every 100.\r\n- During training, choose saved weights by equal-weight real-corpus validation error.\r\n  Generated voices are diagnostic only. The starting weights remain eligible.\r\n- Compare both exported candidates and the original using the application's same\r\n  five-candidate decoding search and compressed numerical format, on all 1,632 real\r\n  validation recordings. Choose the lowest average error, with neither real corpus\r\n  worsening more than 0.5 percentage points. The original model remains eligible.\r\n- Freeze that choice before new test inference. Reuse original baseline test transcripts.\r\n  This historical test has been examined in previous work; it is not a new blind test.\r\n- No new application model is installed automatically. All new weights and transcripts\r\n  stay in `checkpoints/voice_20261110`; originals stay in the existing local voice cache.\r\n\r\nThe exact protocol, input fingerprints and trial outputs are in\r\n`ml_pipeline/reports/voice_tuning_20261110`. Run the study with:\r\n\r\n```powershell\r\nvenv/Scripts/python.exe -u scripts/experiment_voice_tuning.py\r\n```\r\n\r\n## Why these changes\r\n\r\nThis is a real training correctness problem: the old loop discarded the last incomplete\r\ngroup of recordings in an epoch, while its resume calculation assumed that group had\r\nbeen processed. It also omitted numerical-scaling and random-generator state when\r\nsaving progress. The revised trainer processes the tail, records its position explicitly,\r\nsaves those states, passes background-loading errors to the main thread, and keeps the\r\ninitial weights when further training fails to improve validation. New output-directory\r\narguments make it possible to retain every old trained model while experimenting.\r\n\r\nThe two learning rates, trial budget and validation guard are our own design, justified\r\nby this experiment's measurements; they are not claimed to be universally best.\r\n\r\n[Hugging Face's Whisper training guide](https://huggingface.co/blog/fine-tune-whisper)\r\ndocuments supervised speech fine-tuning and word-error measurement.\r\n[The Whisper-ATC paper](https://research.tudelft.nl/en/publications/whisper-atc-open-models-for-air-traffic-control-automatic-speech-/)\r\nreports benefits from radio-specific training and different results for random versus\r\nspeaker-separated tests, supporting the need to retain speaker separation.\r\n[The UWB dataset card](https://huggingface.co/datasets/Jzuluaga/uwb_atcc)\r\nidentifies the existing real radio source and its noncommercial share-alike licence.\r\n\r\nExisting data already provides 17,502 real training clips and 3,341 generated Indian-accent\r\ntraining clips. The 416 generated validation calls use held-out voices and aircraft.\r\nThey cannot establish performance on real Indian controllers. Svarah remains test-only\r\nand measures ordinary Indian English, which differs from aviation radio speech.\r\n\r\nAdditional sources were researched, without adding any recordings to these frozen trials:\r\n[ATCO2's official release](https://atco2.org/data) identifies its free one-hour sample as\r\ndevelopment/evaluation material, and routes the large training corpus through ELRA.\r\n[ATCCaps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22399) reports 202.94 hours but derives its evaluation\r\nsubset from ATCO2, so it would need an overlap audit before use here.\r\n[CABank's ATC collection](https://talkbank.org/ca/access/ATC.html) offers 20 ordinary US\r\nairport recordings with transcripts; its separate disaster subset uses automatic\r\ntranscripts. It is a possible future independent source after checking access terms,\r\naudio segmentation and overlap. No new dataset or real Indian ATC coverage is claimed.\r\n\r\n## Results\r\n\r\nBoth training runs completed 300 updates. The averaged model won the full validation\r\ncomparison at 11.92% / 10.41% word error versus the original's 12.10% / 10.64%.\r\nIts selection was frozen before testing on 12,053 historical recordings. Final testing is complete. Results are modest and mixed, as shown below.\r\n\r\n| Recording collection | Clips | Previous word mistakes per 100 | Selected word mistakes per 100 |\r\n| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: |\r\n| Separate real-radio collection (ATCO2 clean) | 762 | 28.29 | 27.95 |\r\n| Simulation recordings, separate speakers (ATCOSIM) | 1712 | 4.88 | 4.8 |\r\n| Separate radio recordings (UWB) | 2814 | 15.11 | 15.33 |\r\n| Everyday Indian English (Svarah; not radio) | 6656 | 33.17 | 31.46 |\r\n\r\nComplete annotated instructions: 20/31 -> 21/31. The instruction sample is small. The application's default model remains unchanged.\r\n\r\n[Model card and loading instructions](../../ml_pipeline/reports/voice_tuning_20261110/MODEL_CARD.md) \u00b7 [Exact test metrics](../../ml_pipeline/reports/voice_tuning_20261110/test.json) \u00b7 [Uncertainty estimates](../../ml_pipeline/reports/voice_tuning_20261110/uncertainty.json)\r\n\r\n### Validation-only extension before opening the final test\r\n\r\nThe full production-decoder validation comparison put the original at 12.10% / 10.64%\r\nword error (ATCOSIM / UWB), versus the real-only continuation's 12.08% / 10.79%.\r\nThe Indian-accent run improved generated-voice error dramatically, but its step-200\r\nrecorded-speech validation worsened. Its best recorded-speech checkpoint was step 100.\r\nThese are genuine mixed results, not evidence of a broad recognition improvement.\r\n\r\nBefore any final test inference, added one more candidate: an equal full-weight\r\naverage of the original and the two fine-tuned exports. The protocol amendment is\r\ntimestamped in `protocol.json`; the validation selection rule is unchanged.\r\n`ml_pipeline/voice/blend_whisper.py` preserves tied weights, keeps identical tensors\r\nexact, checks shapes, and verifies all three source-file fingerprints remain unchanged.\r\nIt produces one normal Whisper model, not three models running at inference time.\r\n[Wortsman et al.'s model-soups paper](https://proceedings.mlr.press/v162/wortsman22a.html)\r\nmotivates weight averaging from a shared starting model. Its image/text experiments\r\nare not proof that this speech candidate will improve; our validation decides that.\r\nThe equal weights and this adaptive candidate are our experimental choices.\r\n\r\nThe coordinator was restarted after training and export completed, while only the\r\nIndian-accent validation cache contained 100 clips. No training progress was lost;\r\nthose predictions were reused. No final selection or test report existed then.\r\n\r\n### Speed experiment retained as a negative result\r\n\r\nThe library supports independent concurrent worker streams\r\n([CTranslate2 documentation](https://github.com/OpenNMT/CTranslate2/blob/master/docs/parallel.md)).\r\nThe report's `check_parallel_parity.py` tested four workers on 145 validation clips\r\nfor each of two models, including very short and very long clips. All 290 normalized\r\ntranscripts and confidence values matched saved single-worker results exactly.\r\nWall times were 78.77 and 117.62 seconds, versus cached single-clip time sums 85.49\r\nand 89.00 seconds. The runs had concurrent study load and are not isolated speed\r\nbenchmarks. There was no consistent speed benefit, so the optional production-code\r\nworker changes were removed. Final scoring retains the original sequential decoder.\r\n\r\nAn isolated batch-eight implementation was also tried without writing prediction\r\ncaches. The first probe had an incorrect feature-length formula; its source and\r\nfailed results remain as `batch_initial_source.py` and `batch_parity.json`.\r\nAfter correcting that formula, the implementation still changed normalized text on\r\n14/128 original-model clips, 13/128 continuation clips and 14/128 accent-model clips.\r\nIt was rejected for this comparison. These are results for our experimental code,\r\nnot a claim that the library's batch mode is faulty. Official evaluation was unaffected.\r\n\r\n### Generated practice voices: separate development diagnostic\r\n\r\nAfter freezing the model choice, `evaluate_practice_voices.py` scored all 416 generated\r\nvalidation calls with the same runtime decoder for the original and selected models.\r\nThe generated voices and aircraft identities are disjoint from training. Word error\r\nfell from 24.62% to 16.60%. This does not measure real Indian controllers, and it is\r\nnot added to the real-recording test score. Predictions and the exact settings are\r\nretained beside `practice_diagnostic.json`.\r\n\r\n## Reliability check after final testing\r\n\r\n`scripts/report_voice_uncertainty.py` compares old and selected predictions on the\r\nsame test clips, then resamples complete speakers or recording sessions 2,000 times\r\nto show how sensitive the measured difference is to the sampled groups. It never\r\nselects a model. This is a paired cluster percentile bootstrap with seed 42; the\r\ngroup choices and repetition count are our implementation choices.\r\n[Liu and Peng's blockwise-bootstrap paper](https://arxiv.org/abs/1912.09508)\r\nexplains why related utterances should be resampled together, and\r\n[Bisani and Ney's speech-evaluation work](https://www-i6.informatik.rwth-aachen.de/~bisani/bootlog.html)\r\ndemonstrates bootstrap comparisons of word error, including speaker-level analysis.\r\nOur implementation uses percentile intervals, not BootLog's bootstrap-t calculation.\r\nATCOSIM has only three test speakers, so its interval is especially limited.\r\nSvarah has no speaker identities and does not get a claimed speaker-level interval.\r\nThese are descriptive intervals on a historical test, not guarantees of future gains.\r\n"
    },

    {
      "path": "ml_pipeline/reports/voice_tuning_20261110/MODEL_CARD.md",
      "sha256": "d0c3a97fab5efcc3e367e74714dd0994ba887cef7833870124e48f878b8064d1",
      "bytes": 3348,
      "encoding": "utf-8",
      "content": "# Flight Right speech model \u2014 10 November 2026\r\n\r\nSelected version: **uniform_soup3**. Turns short English radio recordings into text.\r\n\r\nTwo additional training trials were compared with the original model and one averaged model. All four were compared on the same 1,632 validation recordings using the application's unchanged decoder. The choice was fixed before the selected model received new final-test inference.\r\n\r\nA fresh original-model check on 256 test recordings across all four collections matched the cached original transcripts and confidence values exactly.\r\n\r\nFinal-test results are mixed: some collections improve and another worsens. Every collection is reported separately below.\r\n\r\n| Recording collection | Clips | Previous word mistakes per 100 | Selected word mistakes per 100 |\r\n| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: |\r\n| Separate real-radio collection (ATCO2 clean) | 762 | 28.29 | 27.95 |\r\n| Simulation recordings, separate speakers (ATCOSIM) | 1712 | 4.88 | 4.8 |\r\n| Separate radio recordings (UWB) | 2814 | 15.11 | 15.33 |\r\n| Everyday Indian English (Svarah; not radio) | 6656 | 33.17 | 31.46 |\r\n\r\nComplete annotated instructions understood correctly: **20/31 \u2192 21/31**. This is a small sample, with known annotation limitations recorded in the evaluation ledger.\r\n\r\nWord mistakes count omitted, added or substituted words per 100 reference words. They do not measure the percentage of whole sentences correct.\r\n\r\nThese are historical evaluation recordings used in previous project work, not a new blind benchmark. Real Indian controller radio accuracy remains unmeasured: generated practice voices and everyday Indian English are different tasks. Speaker/recording resampling uncertainty is in uncertainty.json; the simulation test has only three speakers.\r\n\r\n## Separate generated-voice diagnostic\r\n\r\nOn 416 generated Indian-accent practice recordings, word error was 24.62% \u2192 16.6%. The voices and aircraft identities were kept out of training. This is a development diagnostic on generated audio, not a measurement of real Indian controller radio accuracy.\r\n\r\n## Files and use\r\n\r\nRuntime model folder: `C:\\Users\\singh\\OneDrive\\Desktop\\Archit\\KJ Somaiya\\LY Project\\Flight_Right\\checkpoints\\voice_20261110\\ct2\\local__uniform_soup3__int8_float16`\r\n\r\nFull-precision training/export folder: `C:\\Users\\singh\\OneDrive\\Desktop\\Archit\\KJ Somaiya\\LY Project\\Flight_Right\\checkpoints\\voice_20261110\\models\\uniform_soup3`\r\n\r\nThe running application's model configuration has not been changed by this study. The runtime folder was loaded directly and reproduced an evaluated validation transcript.\r\n\r\nFrom the project environment:\r\n\r\n```python\r\nfrom ml_pipeline.voice.transcribe import load_model, DECODING\r\nmodel = load_model(\"PATH_TO_RUNTIME_FOLDER\", device=\"auto\")\r\nsegments, _ = model.transcribe(\"recording.wav\", **DECODING)\r\nprint(\" \".join(segment.text.strip() for segment in segments))\r\n```\r\n\r\nRuntime format: CTranslate2 int8_float16; CPU loading uses int8. The full Whisper export is also retained for future training.\r\n\r\nTraining includes UWB-ATCC data under CC BY-NC-SA 4.0; this is a noncommercial research artifact. Source: https://huggingface.co/datasets/Jzuluaga/uwb_atcc\r\n\r\nRecorded conflict-detection early catches remain 270/284 (95.1%), not remeasured. This study did not change conflict models or alert thresholds.\r\n"

    {
      "path": "ml_pipeline/reports/voice_tuning_20261110/batch_initial_source.py",
      "sha256": "a707fe6274944b22dcb511b7d68ab060751d52a5b87144e8a5375d7192d5d47f",
      "bytes": 4108,
      "encoding": "utf-8",
      "content": "\"\"\"Exploratory batched execution, without changing any saved prediction cache.\"\"\"\nfrom pathlib import Path\nimport json\nimport sys\nimport time\nimport numpy as np\nimport pandas as pd\n\nROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]\nsys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))\nfrom ml_pipeline.voice import transcribe as tr\nfrom ml_pipeline.voice.train_whisper import all_rows, Audio\nfrom ml_pipeline.voice.build_audio_cache import CACHE, SR\nfrom ml_pipeline.voice.text_norm import normalise\n\n\ndef batch(model, arrays):\n    from faster_whisper import BatchedInferencePipeline\n    from faster_whisper.tokenizer import Tokenizer\n    from faster_whisper.audio import pad_or_trim\n    _, info = model.transcribe(arrays[0], **tr.DECODING)\n    tokenizer = Tokenizer(model.hf_tokenizer, model.model.is_multilingual, task=\"transcribe\", language=\"en\")\n    features = []\n    for audio in arrays:\n        feature = model.feature_extractor(audio)\n        count = feature.shape[-1] - model.feature_extractor.nb_max_frames\n        features.append(pad_or_trim(feature[:, :count]))\n    metadata = [{\"duration\": len(audio) / SR, \"offset\": 0.0} for audio in arrays]\n    outputs = BatchedInferencePipeline(model).forward(np.stack(features), tokenizer, metadata, info.transcription_options)\n    results = []\n    for segments in outputs:\n        if segments and info.transcription_options.no_speech_threshold is not None:\n            skip = segments[0][\"no_speech_prob\"] > info.transcription_options.no_speech_threshold\n            if info.transcription_options.log_prob_threshold is not None and segments[0][\"avg_logprob\"] > info.transcription_options.log_prob_threshold:\n                skip = False\n            if skip:\n                segments = []\n        results.append({\"hyp\": \" \".join(s[\"text\"].strip() for s in segments).strip(),\n                        \"avg_logprob\": float(np.mean([s[\"avg_logprob\"] for s in segments])) if segments else float(\"nan\")})\n    return results\n\n\ndef main():\n    work = ROOT / \"checkpoints/voice_20261110\"\n    baseline = str(CACHE / \"models/flightright-whisper-small-atc-p1\")\n    original_ct2, original_path = tr.CT2_DIR, tr.ct2_path\n    tr.CT2_DIR = work / \"ct2\"\n    tr.ct2_path = lambda model, quant: (original_ct2 / f\"{tr._slug(model)}__{quant}\" if model == baseline else original_path(model, quant))\n    rows = all_rows()\n    val = rows[(rows.split == \"val\") & rows.corpus.isin([\"atcosim\", \"uwb\"])]\n    subset = pd.concat([f.sample(64, random_state=42) for _, f in val.groupby(\"corpus\")])\n    if \"--full\" in sys.argv:\n        subset = val\n    audio = Audio()\n    report = {\"batch_size\": 8, \"models\": {}, \"scope\": \"Output parity only; no test data or model selection; concurrent study load.\"}\n    models = {\"baseline\": baseline, \"continue_lr1e4\": str(work / \"models/continue_lr1e4\"),\n              \"indian_mix_lr5e5\": str(work / \"models/indian_mix_lr5e5\")}\n    for name, model_id in models.items():\n        reference = pd.read_parquet(work / \"transcripts\" / f\"{tr._slug(model_id)}__int8_float16.parquet\").set_index(\"uid\")\n        selected = subset[subset.uid.isin(reference.index)]\n        model = tr.load_model(model_id, device=\"cuda\")\n        started, got = time.perf_counter(), []\n        for start in range(0, len(selected), 8):\n            frame = selected.iloc[start:start + 8]\n            got += list(zip(frame.uid, batch(model, [audio(row) for row in frame.itertuples()])))\n        elapsed = time.perf_counter() - started\n        mismatches = [uid for uid, value in got if normalise(value[\"hyp\"]) != normalise(reference.loc[uid, \"hyp\"])]\n        differences = [abs(value[\"avg_logprob\"] - reference.loc[uid, \"avg_logprob\"]) for uid, value in got]\n        report[\"models\"][name] = {\"clips\": len(got), \"text_mismatch_uids\": mismatches,\n            \"max_abs_logprob_difference\": float(np.nanmax(differences)), \"wall_seconds\": round(elapsed, 2)}\n        print(name, report[\"models\"][name], flush=True)\n        Path(__file__).with_name(\"batch_full_parity.json\" if \"--full\" in sys.argv else \"batch_parity.json\").write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2))\n        del model\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n"
    },

    {
      "path": "ml_pipeline/reports/voice_tuning_20261110/batch_parity_corrected.log",
      "sha256": "9179dae143bc2a9a6da9a23376751144e05420ef57637058e3ff84d229005cab",
      "bytes": 766,
      "encoding": "base64",
      "content": "//5iAGEAcwBlAGwAaQBuAGUAIAB7ACcAYwBsAGkAcABzACcAOgAgADEAMgA4ACwAIAAnAHQAZQB4AHQAXwBtAGkAcwBtAGEAdABjAGgAXwB1AGkAZABzACcAOgAgADEANAAsACAAJwBtAGEAeABfAGEAYgBzAF8AbABvAGcAcAByAG8AYgBfAGQAaQBmAGYAZQByAGUAbgBjAGUAJwA6ACAAMAAuADEANAA4ADAANAA2ADgANQA1ADEAMwAxADcAOAA1ADAANgAsACAAJwB3AGEAbABsAF8AcwBlAGMAbwBuAGQAcwAnADoAIAAyADIALgA3ADgAfQANAAoAYwBvAG4AdABpAG4AdQBlAF8AbAByADEAZQA0ACAAewAnAGMAbABpAHAAcwAnADoAIAAxADIAOAAsACAAJwB0AGUAeAB0AF8AbQBpAHMAbQBhAHQAYwBoAF8AdQBpAGQAcwAnADoAIAAxADMALAAgACcAbQBhAHgAXwBhAGIAcwBfAGwAbwBnAHAAcgBvAGIAXwBkAGkAZgBmAGUAcgBlAG4AYwBlACcAOgAgADAALgAwADgANgA0ADIANQA3ADgAMQAyADUALAAgACcAdwBhAGwAbABfAHMAZQBjAG8AbgBkAHMAJwA6ACAAMgAzAC4AMQAyAH0ADQAKAGkAbgBkAGkAYQBuAF8AbQBpAHgAXwBsAHIANQBlADUAIAB7ACcAYwBsAGkAcABzACcAOgAgADEAMgA4ACwAIAAnAHQAZQB4AHQAXwBtAGkAcwBtAGEAdABjAGgAXwB1AGkAZABzACcAOgAgADEANAAsACAAJwBtAGEAeABfAGEAYgBzAF8AbABvAGcAcAByAG8AYgBfAGQAaQBmAGYAZQByAGUAbgBjAGUAJwA6ACAAMAAuADMAMwA5ADEAOQAyADYANQA3ADcANQA5ADYAOQA1ADMALAAgACcAdwBhAGwAbABfAHMAZQBjAG8AbgBkAHMAJwA6ACAAMgAzAC4ANgA3AH0ADQAKAA=="

    {
      "path": "ml_pipeline/reports/voice_tuning_20261110/check_baseline_cache.py",
      "sha256": "c3a7394bf58ff037edee120b2b33bb7df65d35747d6b4c084cad0bb58850fbac",
      "bytes": 2459,
      "encoding": "utf-8",
      "content": "\"\"\"Fresh original-model checks across all historical test collections, after choice froze.\"\"\"\nfrom pathlib import Path\nimport json\nimport sys\nimport numpy as np\nimport pandas as pd\n\nROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]\nsys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))\nfrom ml_pipeline.voice.build_audio_cache import CACHE\nfrom ml_pipeline.voice.train_whisper import Audio\nfrom ml_pipeline.voice.transcribe import load_model, transcribe_array, ASR_VERSION\nfrom ml_pipeline.voice.text_norm import normalise\n\n\ndef main():\n    report = Path(__file__).resolve().parent\n    assert (report / \"selection.json\").exists()\n    manifest = pd.read_parquet(CACHE / \"manifest.parquet\")\n    manifest = manifest[(manifest.split == \"test\") & manifest.corpus.isin([\"atcosim\", \"uwb\", \"atco2\", \"svarah\"])]\n    cached = pd.read_parquet(CACHE / \"transcripts/local__flightright-whisper-small-atc-p1__int8_float16.parquet\")\n    cached = cached[cached.asr_version == ASR_VERSION].set_index(\"uid\")\n    model_id = str(CACHE / \"models/flightright-whisper-small-atc-p1\")\n    model = load_model(model_id, device=\"cuda\")\n    audio = Audio()\n    output = {\"model\": model_id, \"seed\": 42, \"scope\": \"Fresh original-model cache control; no model-selection changes\", \"corpora\": {}}\n    predictions = []\n    for corpus, frame in manifest.groupby(\"corpus\"):\n        selected = frame.sample(min(64, len(frame)), random_state=42)\n        mismatches, deltas = [], []\n        for row in selected.itertuples():\n            got = transcribe_array(model, audio(row))\n            predictions.append({\"uid\": row.uid, \"corpus\": corpus, **got})\n            if normalise(got[\"hyp\"]) != normalise(cached.loc[row.uid, \"hyp\"]):\n                mismatches.append(row.uid)\n            deltas.append(abs(got[\"avg_logprob\"] - cached.loc[row.uid, \"avg_logprob\"]))\n        output[\"corpora\"][corpus] = {\"clips\": len(selected), \"text_mismatch_uids\": mismatches,\n                                      \"max_abs_logprob_difference\": float(np.nanmax(deltas))}\n        (report / \"baseline_cache_control.json\").write_text(json.dumps(output, indent=2), encoding=\"utf-8\")\n        pd.DataFrame(predictions).to_parquet(report / \"baseline_cache_control_predictions.parquet\", index=False)\n        print(corpus, output[\"corpora\"][corpus], flush=True)\n    assert all(not values[\"text_mismatch_uids\"] for values in output[\"corpora\"].values()), \"Cached original-model outputs changed; rerun its full benchmark\"\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "ml_pipeline/reports/voice_tuning_20261110/check_batch_parity.py",
      "sha256": "f631143726b12dcd25df2dea197a398046dfb6f80ed40729b5b14a572ecc4b5b",
      "bytes": 4125,
      "encoding": "utf-8",
      "content": "\"\"\"Exploratory batched execution, without changing any saved prediction cache.\"\"\"\nfrom pathlib import Path\nimport json\nimport sys\nimport time\nimport numpy as np\nimport pandas as pd\n\nROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]\nsys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))\nfrom ml_pipeline.voice import transcribe as tr\nfrom ml_pipeline.voice.train_whisper import all_rows, Audio\nfrom ml_pipeline.voice.build_audio_cache import CACHE, SR\nfrom ml_pipeline.voice.text_norm import normalise\n\n\ndef batch(model, arrays):\n    from faster_whisper import BatchedInferencePipeline\n    from faster_whisper.tokenizer import Tokenizer\n    from faster_whisper.audio import pad_or_trim\n    _, info = model.transcribe(arrays[0], **tr.DECODING)\n    tokenizer = Tokenizer(model.hf_tokenizer, model.model.is_multilingual, task=\"transcribe\", language=\"en\")\n    features = []\n    for audio in arrays:\n        feature = model.feature_extractor(audio)\n        count = feature.shape[-1] - 1\n        features.append(pad_or_trim(feature[:, :count]))\n    metadata = [{\"duration\": len(audio) / SR, \"offset\": 0.0} for audio in arrays]\n    outputs = BatchedInferencePipeline(model).forward(np.stack(features), tokenizer, metadata, info.transcription_options)\n    results = []\n    for segments in outputs:\n        if segments and info.transcription_options.no_speech_threshold is not None:\n            skip = segments[0][\"no_speech_prob\"] > info.transcription_options.no_speech_threshold\n            if info.transcription_options.log_prob_threshold is not None and segments[0][\"avg_logprob\"] > info.transcription_options.log_prob_threshold:\n                skip = False\n            if skip:\n                segments = []\n        results.append({\"hyp\": \" \".join(s[\"text\"].strip() for s in segments).strip(),\n                        \"avg_logprob\": float(np.mean([s[\"avg_logprob\"] for s in segments])) if segments else float(\"nan\")})\n    return results\n\n\ndef main():\n    work = ROOT / \"checkpoints/voice_20261110\"\n    baseline = str(CACHE / \"models/flightright-whisper-small-atc-p1\")\n    original_ct2, original_path = tr.CT2_DIR, tr.ct2_path\n    tr.CT2_DIR = work / \"ct2\"\n    tr.ct2_path = lambda model, quant: (original_ct2 / f\"{tr._slug(model)}__{quant}\" if model == baseline else original_path(model, quant))\n    rows = all_rows()\n    val = rows[(rows.split == \"val\") & rows.corpus.isin([\"atcosim\", \"uwb\"])]\n    subset = pd.concat([f.sample(64, random_state=42) for _, f in val.groupby(\"corpus\")])\n    if \"--full\" in sys.argv:\n        subset = val\n    audio = Audio()\n    report = {\"batch_size\": 8, \"models\": {}, \"scope\": \"Output parity only; no test data or model selection; concurrent study load.\"}\n    models = {\"baseline\": baseline, \"continue_lr1e4\": str(work / \"models/continue_lr1e4\"),\n              \"indian_mix_lr5e5\": str(work / \"models/indian_mix_lr5e5\")}\n    for name, model_id in models.items():\n        reference = pd.read_parquet(work / \"transcripts\" / f\"{tr._slug(model_id)}__int8_float16.parquet\").set_index(\"uid\")\n        selected = subset[subset.uid.isin(reference.index)]\n        model = tr.load_model(model_id, device=\"cuda\")\n        started, got = time.perf_counter(), []\n        for start in range(0, len(selected), 8):\n            frame = selected.iloc[start:start + 8]\n            got += list(zip(frame.uid, batch(model, [audio(row) for row in frame.itertuples()])))\n        elapsed = time.perf_counter() - started\n        mismatches = [uid for uid, value in got if normalise(value[\"hyp\"]) != normalise(reference.loc[uid, \"hyp\"])]\n        differences = [abs(value[\"avg_logprob\"] - reference.loc[uid, \"avg_logprob\"]) for uid, value in got]\n        report[\"models\"][name] = {\"clips\": len(got), \"text_mismatch_uids\": mismatches,\n            \"max_abs_logprob_difference\": float(np.nanmax(differences)), \"wall_seconds\": round(elapsed, 2)}\n        print(name, {**report[\"models\"][name], \"text_mismatch_uids\": len(mismatches)}, flush=True)\n        Path(__file__).with_name(\"batch_full_parity.json\" if \"--full\" in sys.argv else \"batch_parity_corrected.json\").write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2))\n        del model\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "ml_pipeline/reports/voice_tuning_20261110/check_parallel_parity.py",
      "sha256": "a86cdca966ecb5ba832b9fc1085a6b23a2bc0123f3b8cda75647e7499dda0b05",
      "bytes": 3024,
      "encoding": "utf-8",
      "content": "\"\"\"Compare unchanged single-clip decoder under four worker streams with saved results.\"\"\"\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor\nimport json\nimport sys\nimport time\nimport numpy as np\nimport pandas as pd\n\nROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]\nsys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))\nfrom ml_pipeline.voice import transcribe as tr\nfrom ml_pipeline.voice.train_whisper import all_rows, Audio\nfrom ml_pipeline.voice.build_audio_cache import CACHE\nfrom ml_pipeline.voice.text_norm import normalise\n\nwork = ROOT / \"checkpoints/voice_20261110\"\nbaseline = str(CACHE / \"models/flightright-whisper-small-atc-p1\")\noriginal_ct2 = tr.CT2_DIR\noriginal_path = tr.ct2_path\ntr.CT2_DIR = work / \"ct2\"\ntr.ct2_path = lambda model, quant: (original_ct2 / f\"{tr._slug(model)}__{quant}\"\n                                  if model == baseline else original_path(model, quant))\nrows = all_rows()\nval = rows[(rows.split == \"val\") & rows.corpus.isin([\"atcosim\", \"uwb\"])]\npieces = []\nfor _, frame in val.groupby(\"corpus\"):\n    pieces += [frame.sample(64, random_state=42), frame.nsmallest(5, \"n_samples\"), frame.nlargest(5, \"n_samples\")]\nsubset = pd.concat(pieces).drop_duplicates(\"uid\")\naudio = Audio()\n# Pre-open memory maps before sharing read-only access across threads.\naudio(next(subset.itertuples()))\nreport = {\"workers\": 4, \"decoding\": tr.DECODING,\n          \"note\": \"Same per-clip transcribe_array and decoding; timing under concurrent study load is not an isolated latency benchmark.\", \"models\": {}}\nfor name, model_id in {\"baseline\": baseline, \"continue_lr1e4\": str(work / \"models/continue_lr1e4\")}.items():\n    reference = pd.read_parquet(work / \"transcripts\" / f\"{tr._slug(model_id)}__int8_float16.parquet\").set_index(\"uid\")\n    from faster_whisper import WhisperModel\n    tr._cuda_libraries_on_path()\n    model = WhisperModel(str(tr.ct2_path(model_id, \"int8_float16\")), device=\"cuda\",\n                         compute_type=\"int8_float16\", num_workers=4)\n    def recognise(row):\n        return row.uid, tr.transcribe_array(model, audio(row))\n    started = time.perf_counter()\n    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=4) as pool:\n        got = list(pool.map(recognise, subset.itertuples()))\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - started\n    mismatches = [uid for uid, value in got if normalise(value[\"hyp\"]) != normalise(reference.loc[uid, \"hyp\"])]\n    deltas = [abs(value[\"avg_logprob\"] - reference.loc[uid, \"avg_logprob\"]) for uid, value in got]\n    entry = {\"clips\": len(got), \"text_mismatch_uids\": mismatches,\n             \"max_abs_logprob_difference\": float(np.nanmax(deltas)), \"wall_seconds\": round(elapsed, 2),\n             \"cached_single_clip_seconds_sum\": round(float(reference.loc[subset.uid, \"seconds\"].sum()), 2)}\n    report[\"models\"][name] = entry\n    print(name, entry, flush=True)\n    Path(__file__).with_name(\"parallel_parity.json\").write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2))\n    del model\nassert all(not value[\"text_mismatch_uids\"] for value in report[\"models\"].values()), \"Decoder parity failed\"\n"
    },
    {

      "bytes": 2873,
      "encoding": "utf-8",
      "content": "\"\"\"Development diagnostic only: generated held-out Indian-accent voices, not real radio.\"\"\"\nfrom pathlib import Path\nimport json\nimport sys\nimport time\nimport pandas as pd\n\nROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]\nsys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))\nfrom ml_pipeline.voice import transcribe as tr\nfrom ml_pipeline.voice.train_whisper import all_rows, Audio\nfrom ml_pipeline.voice.build_audio_cache import CACHE\nfrom ml_pipeline.voice.evaluate_voice import wer\nfrom ml_pipeline.voice.text_norm import normalise\n\n\ndef main():\n    report = Path(__file__).resolve().parent\n    selection = json.loads((report / \"selection.json\").read_text())\n    rows = all_rows()\n    training = rows[(rows.corpus == \"tts_indian\") & (rows.split == \"train\")]\n    val = rows[(rows.corpus == \"tts_indian\") & (rows.split == \"val\")]\n    assert not set(training.speaker) & set(val.speaker)\n    assert not set(training.group) & set(val.group)\n    original = str(CACHE / \"models/flightright-whisper-small-atc-p1\")\n    original_ct2, original_path = tr.CT2_DIR, tr.ct2_path\n    tr.CT2_DIR = ROOT / \"checkpoints/voice_20261110/ct2\"\n    tr.ct2_path = lambda model, quant: (original_ct2 / f\"{tr._slug(model)}__{quant}\" if model == original else original_path(model, quant))\n    output = {\"scope\": \"Generated practice voices only; development diagnostic, not evidence of real Indian ATC accuracy\",\n              \"clips\": len(val), \"held_out_generated_voices\": sorted(val.speaker.unique()),\n              \"selection_already_frozen\": selection[\"frozen_utc\"], \"decoding\": tr.DECODING, \"models\": {}}\n    audio = Audio()\n    for name, model_id in {\"baseline\": original, \"selected\": selection[\"model\"]}.items():\n        cache = report / f\"practice_{name}_predictions.parquet\"\n        done = pd.read_parquet(cache) if cache.exists() else pd.DataFrame(columns=[\"uid\", \"hyp\"])\n        todo = val[~val.uid.isin(done.uid)]\n        if len(todo):\n            model = tr.load_model(model_id, device=\"cuda\")\n            got = []\n            for n, row in enumerate(todo.itertuples(), 1):\n                result = tr.transcribe_array(model, audio(row))\n                got.append({\"uid\": row.uid, **result})\n                if n % 50 == 0:\n                    pd.concat([done, pd.DataFrame(got)]).to_parquet(cache, index=False)\n                    print(name, n, \"/\", len(todo), flush=True)\n            done = pd.concat([done, pd.DataFrame(got)], ignore_index=True)\n            done.to_parquet(cache, index=False)\n            del model\n        hypotheses = done.set_index(\"uid\").loc[val.uid].hyp\n        output[\"models\"][name] = {\"model\": model_id, \"word_error_percent\": wer(list(val.text), [normalise(h) for h in hypotheses])}\n        (report / \"practice_diagnostic.json\").write_text(json.dumps(output, indent=2), encoding=\"utf-8\")\n        print(name, output[\"models\"][name], flush=True)\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "ml_pipeline/reports/voice_tuning_20261110/finalize.log",

AYQBsAHMAbwAgAHIAZQB0AGEAaQBuAGUAZAAgAGYAbwByACAAZgB1AHQAdQByAGUAIAB0AHIAYQBpAG4AaQBuAGcALgANAAoADQAKAFQAcgBhAGkAbgBpAG4AZwAgAGkAbgBjAGwAdQBkAGUAcwAgAFUAVwBCAC0AQQBUAEMAQwAgAGQAYQB0AGEAIAB1AG4AZABlAHIAIABDAEMAIABCAFkALQBOAEMALQBTAEEAIAA0AC4AMAA7ACAAdABoAGkAcwAgAGkAcwAgAGEAIABuAG8AbgBjAG8AbQBtAGUAcgBjAGkAYQBsACAAcgBlAHMAZQBhAHIAYwBoACAAYQByAHQAaQBmAGEAYwB0AC4AIABTAG8AdQByAGMAZQA6ACAAaAB0AHQAcABzADoALwAvAGgAdQBnAGcAaQBuAGcAZgBhAGMAZQAuAGMAbwAvAGQAYQB0AGEAcwBlAHQAcwAvAEoAegB1AGwAdQBhAGcAYQAvAHUAdwBiAF8AYQB0AGMAYwANAAoADQAKAFIAZQBjAG8AcgBkAGUAZAAgAGMAbwBuAGYAbABpAGMAdAAtAGQAZQB0AGUAYwB0AGkAbwBuACAAZQBhAHIAbAB5ACAAYwBhAHQAYwBoAGUAcwAgAHIAZQBtAGEAaQBuACAAMgA3ADAALwAyADgANAAgACgAOQA1AC4AMQAlACkALAAgAG4AbwB0ACAAcgBlAG0AZQBhAHMAdQByAGUAZAAuACAAVABoAGkAcwAgAHMAdAB1AGQAeQAgAGQAaQBkACAAbgBvAHQAIABjAGgAYQBuAGcAZQAgAGMAbwBuAGYAbABpAGMAdAAgAG0AbwBkAGUAbABzACAAbwByACAAYQBsAGUAcgB0ACAAdABoAHIAZQBzAGgAbwBsAGQAcwAuAA0ACgANAAoA"

    {
      "path": "ml_pipeline/reports/voice_tuning_20261110/finalize.py",
      "sha256": "be6d41e18388f52eedd0906d78dabbddb7388e143448900945fb28ccbac2fb22",
      "bytes": 14968,
      "encoding": "utf-8",
      "content": "\"\"\"Finalize the model card and evidence only after frozen-choice testing completes.\"\"\"\nfrom pathlib import Path\nimport hashlib\nimport json\nimport subprocess\nimport sys\nfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\n\nROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]\nsys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))\nREPORT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent\nWORK = ROOT / \"checkpoints/voice_20261110\"\n\n\ndef sha(path):\n    return hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()\n\n\ndef main():\n    selection = json.loads((REPORT / \"selection.json\").read_text())\n    results = json.loads((REPORT / \"test.json\").read_text())\n    uncertainty = json.loads((REPORT / \"uncertainty.json\").read_text())\n    control = json.loads((REPORT / \"baseline_cache_control.json\").read_text())\n    assert set(control[\"corpora\"]) == {\"atco2\", \"atcosim\", \"uwb\", \"svarah\"}\n    assert all(not item[\"text_mismatch_uids\"] for item in control[\"corpora\"].values())\n    assert results[\"winner\"] == selection[\"winner\"]\n    old, new = results[\"baseline\"][\"corpora\"], results[\"selected\"][\"corpora\"]\n    for short, key in {\"atco2_clean\": \"atco2_clean\", \"atcosim\": \"atcosim_heldout_speakers\", \"uwb\": \"uwb_heldout_recordings\"}.items():\n        counts = uncertainty[\"cohorts\"][short]\n        for errors, score in ((counts[\"previous_errors\"], old[key][\"wer\"]), (counts[\"selected_errors\"], new[key][\"wer\"])):\n            assert abs(100 * errors / counts[\"reference_words\"] - score) < 0.006\n    runtime = Path(selection[\"runtime_model\"])\n    import pandas as pd\n    from ml_pipeline.voice.build_audio_cache import CACHE\n    from ml_pipeline.voice.train_whisper import Audio\n    from ml_pipeline.voice.transcribe import load_model, transcribe_array, _slug\n    from ml_pipeline.voice.text_norm import normalise\n    manifest = pd.read_parquet(CACHE / \"manifest.parquet\")\n    row = next(manifest[(manifest.split == \"val\") & (manifest.corpus == \"atcosim\")].itertuples())\n    model = load_model(str(runtime), device=\"cuda\")\n    actual = transcribe_array(model, Audio()(row))\n    cache_root = CACHE / \"transcripts\" if selection[\"winner\"] == \"baseline\" else WORK / \"transcripts\"\n    saved = pd.read_parquet(cache_root / f\"{_slug(selection['model'])}__int8_float16.parquet\").set_index(\"uid\")\n    assert normalise(actual[\"hyp\"]) == normalise(saved.loc[row.uid, \"hyp\"]), \"Direct runtime-folder inference differs\"\n    (REPORT / \"runtime_check.json\").write_text(json.dumps({\"uid\": row.uid, \"model\": str(runtime),\n        \"same_words_as_evaluated_export\": True, \"hyp\": actual[\"hyp\"]}, indent=2), encoding=\"utf-8\")\n\n    labels = {\"atco2_clean\": \"Separate real-radio collection (ATCO2 clean)\",\n              \"atcosim_heldout_speakers\": \"Simulation recordings, separate speakers (ATCOSIM)\",\n              \"uwb_heldout_recordings\": \"Separate radio recordings (UWB)\",\n              \"svarah_indian_english\": \"Everyday Indian English (Svarah; not radio)\"}\n    table = [\"| Recording collection | Clips | Previous word mistakes per 100 | Selected word mistakes per 100 |\",\n             \"| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: |\"]\n    for key, label in labels.items():\n        table.append(f\"| {label} | {new[key]['clips']} | {old[key]['wer']} | {new[key]['wer']} |\")\n    n = new[\"atco2_clean\"][\"clearance_n\"]\n    before = Turns short English radio recordings into text.\", \"\",\n            \"Two additional training trials were compared with the original model and one averaged model. \"\n            \"All four were compared on the same 1,632 validation recordings using the application's unchanged decoder. \"\n            \"The choice was fixed before the selected model received new final-test inference.\", \"\",\n            \"A fresh original-model check on 256 test recordings across all four collections \"\n            \"matched the cached original transcripts and confidence values exactly.\", \"\",\n            \"Final-test results are mixed: some collections improve and another worsens. \"\n            \"Every collection is reported separately below.\", \"\",\n            *table, \"\", f\"Complete annotated instructions understood correctly: **{before}/{n} \u2192 {after}/{n}**. \"\n            \"This is a small sample, with known annotation limitations recorded in the evaluation ledger.\", \"\",\n            \"Word mistakes count omitted, added or substituted words per 100 reference words. \"\n            \"They do not measure the percentage of whole sentences correct.\", \"\",\n            \"These are historical evaluation recordings used in previous project work, not a new blind benchmark. \"\n            \"Real Indian controller radio accuracy remains unmeasured: generated practice voices and everyday Indian English are different tasks. \"\n            \"Speaker/recording resampling uncertainty is in uncertainty.json; the simulation test has only three speakers.\", \"\",\n            *practice_lines, \"## Files and use\", \"\", f\"Runtime model folder: `{runtime}`\", \"\",\n            f\"Full-precision training/export folder: `{selection['model']}`\", \"\",\n            \"The running application's model configuration has not been changed by this study. \"\n            \"The runtime folder was loaded directly and reproduced an evaluated validation transcript.\", \"\",\n            \"From the project environment:\", \"\", \"```python\",\n            \"from ml_pipeline.voice.transcribe import load_model, DECODING\",\n            'model = load_model(\"PATH_TO_RUNTIME_FOLDER\", device=\"auto\")',\n            'segments, _ = model.transcribe(\"recording.wav\", **DECODING)',\n            'print(\" \".join(segment.text.strip() for segment in segments))', \"```\", \"\",\n            \"Runtime format: CTranslate2 int8_float16; CPU loading uses int8. \"\n            \"The full Whisper export is also retained for future training.\", \"\",\n            \"Training includes UWB-ATCC data under CC BY-NC-SA 4.0; this is a noncommercial research artifact. \"\n            \"Source: https://huggingface.co/datasets/Jzuluaga/uwb_atcc\", \"\",\n            \"Recorded conflict-detection early catches remain 270/284 (95.1%), not remeasured. \"\n            \"This study did not change conflict models or alert thresholds.\", \"\"]\n    text = \"\\n\".join(card)\n    (REPORT / \"MODEL_CARD.md\").write_text(text, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n    if runtime.resolve().is_relative_to(WORK.resolve()):\n        (runtime / \"README.md\").write_text(text, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n    artifact = json.loads((REPORT / \"selected_artifact.json\").read_text())\n    artifact[\"files\"] = {p.name: {\"bytes\": p.stat().st_size, \"sha256\": sha(p)} for p in runtime.iterdir() if p.is_file()}\n    (REPORT / \"selected_artifact.json\").write_text(json.dumps(artifact, indent=2), encoding=\"utf-8\")\n    sources = [\"ml_pipeline/voice/train_whisper.py\", \"ml_pipeline/voice/export_whisper.py\",\n               \"ml_pipeline/voice/blend_whisper.py\", \"ml_pipeline/voice/transcribe.py\",\n               \"scripts/experiment_voice_tuning.py\", \"scripts/audit_voice_data.py\",\n               \"scripts/report_voice_uncertainty.py\", \"ml_pipeline/tests/test_voice_training.py\"]\n    snapshot = {\"git_base\": subprocess.check_output([\"git\", \"rev-parse\", \"HEAD\"], cwd=ROOT, text=True).strip(),\n                \"finalized_utc\": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),\n                \"sources_sha256\": {p: sha(ROOT / p) for p in sources},\n                \"unit_test_log_sha256\": sha(REPORT / \"unit_tests.log\"),\n                \"note\": \"Final reproduction source; Results are modest and mixed, as shown below.\\n\\n\" +\n                 \"\\n\".join(table) + f\"\\n\\nComplete annotated instructions: {before}/{n} -> {after}/{n}. \"\n                 \"The instruction sample is small. The application's default model remains unchanged.\\n\\n\"\n                 \"[Model card and loading instructions](../../ml_pipeline/reports/voice_tuning_20261110/MODEL_CARD.md) \u00b7 \"\n                 \"[Exact test metrics](../../ml_pipeline/reports/voice_tuning_20261110/test.json) \u00b7 \"\n                 \"[Uncertainty estimates](../../ml_pipeline/reports/voice_tuning_20261110/uncertainty.json)\")\n    if pending in study:\n        study_path.write_text(study.replace(pending, completed, 1), encoding=\"utf-8\")\n\n    ledger = ROOT / \"docs/evaluations/asr_small_atc_20261110.md\"\n    ledger.write_text(\"# Flight Right Whisper-small continuation study: 10 November 2026\\n\\n\"\n        \"Validation-selected uniform weight average of the original and two continued-training models. \"\n        \"An offline candidate; the application's original speech model remains configured.\\n\\n\"\n        \"[Complete study](../experiments/voice_tuning_20261110.md) \u00b7 \"\n        \"[Model card](../../ml_pipeline/reports/voice_tuning_20261110/MODEL_CARD.md)\\n\\n\" +\n        \"\\n\".join(table) + f\"\\n\\nComplete instructions: {before}/{n} -> {after}/{n}. \"\n        \"These historical test data were used in previous work; results are exploratory. \"\n        \"The synthetic-voice diagnostic is not real Indian radio accuracy.\\n\\n\"\n        \"Exact protocol and its pre-test amendment, every validation trial, all final test metrics, \"\n        \"paired uncertainty estimates, software environment, model-file fingerprints and final source hashes \"\n        \"remain under `ml_pipeline/reports/voice_tuning_20261110/`. New weights and prediction caches remain \"\n        \"local under `checkpoints/voice_20261110/`. No historical evidence is removed or replaced.\\n\",\n        encoding=\"utf-8\")\n    baseline_ledger = ROOT / \"docs/evaluations/asr_small_atc_p1.md\"\n    raw = baseline_ledger.read_bytes()\n    if b\"asr_small_atc_20261110.md\" not in raw:\n        note = b\"[10 November continuation study](asr_small_atc_20261110.md): new offline candidate and mixed held-out results; original app model retained.\\n\\n\"\n        baseline_ledger.write_bytes(raw.replace(b\"[Latest comparison]\", note + b\"[Latest comparison]\", 1))\n\n    heading = \"### 10 November 2026 - Completed speech continuation study and exported candidate\"\n    log_path = ROOT / \"docs/CLAUDE.md\"\n    if heading.encode() not in log_path.read_bytes():\n        summary = \"; \".join(f\"{label}: {old[key]['wer']}% -> {new[key]['wer']}%\" for key, label in labels.items())\n        entry = f\"\\n\\n{heading}\\n\\n\" + (\n            \"Archit requested proper speech-model tuning, permitted research and extra data, and asked for a beginner-friendly explanation. \"\n            \"Completed two 300-update training runs and one uniform weight-average candidate. \"\n            \"The full validation comparison chose uniform_soup3 at 11.92% / 10.41% word error, \"\n            \"against the original's 12.10% / 10.64%, before any new final-test inference. \"\n            \"The averaging candidate was added in a documented validation-only amendment; selection rules stayed fixed.\\n\\n\"\n            \"Files: ml_pipeline/voice/train_whisper.py fixes skipped final groups, saved progress and initial-checkpoint retention; \"\n            \"ml_pipeline/voice/export_whisper.py isolates exports; ml_pipeline/voice/blend_whisper.py averages aligned weights \"\n            \"without changing originals; ml_pipeline/voice/transcribe.py loads ready runtime folders directly. \"\n            \"scripts/experiment_voice_tuning.py runs the study, scripts/audit_voice_data.py checks audio separation, \"\n            \"scripts/report_voice_uncertainty.py estimates sampling uncertainty, and ml_pipeline/tests/test_voice_training.py \"\n            \"provides nine focused checks. All 90 Python tests pass. Study-specific diagnostic/finalization code, \"\n            \"exact outputs and input/source/model fingerprints are preserved in ml_pipeline/reports/voice_tuning_20261110/.\\n\\n\"\n            \"Before this work, unused Indian-accent practice recordings were available, and the trainer could skip incomplete \"\n            \"groups and choose a worse trained model over its starting point. It now preserves the data cursor and random/scaling \"\n            \"state, processes those groups, forwards loading failures and keeps the starting checkpoint eligible. \"\n            \"The audio audit found 34,944 distinct usable waveforms with no exact cross-split duplicates. \"\n            \"No training/validation target exceeded the token limit. A fresh 256-recording baseline control matched old \"\n            \"transcripts and confidence values exactly. Speed alternatives were tested and rejected; official decoding stayed fixed.\\n\\n\"\n        ) + f\"Final test, 12,053 recordings: {summary}. Complete instructions {before}/{n} -> {after}/{n}. \" + (\n            \"These are small, mixed changes, not a universal improvement. Generated practice voices improved from 24.62% \"\n            \"to 16.60% word error on 416 held-out generated calls; that does not measure real Indian controllers. \"\n            \"The historical test has been inspected before, and the simulation corpus has only three test speakers. \"\n            \"The uncertainty file records paired resampling estimates rather than claiming guaranteed future gains.\\n\\n\"\n            \"The new candidate was exported in full and runtime formats, then loaded directly to reproduce a validation transcript. \"\n            \"Its model card and exact file fingerprints identify what to use. docs/experiments/voice_tuning_20261110.md \"\n            \"and docs/evaluations/asr_small_atc_20261110.md record the study; the original speech ledger links it without \"\n            \"rewriting historical evidence. The application's original model stays configured because results are mixed. \"\n            \"No alert threshold, classifier or trajectory weights changed. Recorded early conflict catches remain 270/284 \"\n            \"(95.1%), not remeasured. All changes and new weights remain local and uncommitted.\\n\"\n        )\n        with log_path.open(\"ab\") as log:\n            log.write(entry.encode(\"utf-8\"))\n    print(text)\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n"

    {
      "path": "ml_pipeline/reports/voice_tuning_20261110/precompute_validation.py",
      "sha256": "60766ddff729986f102ab1d55b4fd53ade672b6e4bf1b7db27c48bec69d28a9e",
      "bytes": 1619,
      "encoding": "utf-8",
      "content": "\"\"\"Precompute unchanged validation comparisons while the second trial trains.\n\nNo test inference or model selection. The main driver reuses these same cached rows.\nElapsed inference times under concurrent training are not latency benchmarks.\n\"\"\"\nfrom pathlib import Path\nimport json\nimport sys\n\nROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]\nsys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))\nfrom ml_pipeline.voice import transcribe as tr\nfrom ml_pipeline.voice import evaluate_voice as ev\nfrom ml_pipeline.voice.train_whisper import all_rows\nfrom ml_pipeline.voice.build_audio_cache import CACHE\nfrom ml_pipeline.voice.text_norm import normalise\n\nWORK = ROOT / \"checkpoints/voice_20261110\"\nbaseline = str(CACHE / \"models/flightright-whisper-small-atc-p1\")\nmodels = {\"baseline\": baseline, \"continue_lr1e4\": str(WORK / \"models/continue_lr1e4\")}\noriginal_ct2 = tr.CT2_DIR\noriginal_path = tr.ct2_path\ntr.CT2_DIR = WORK / \"ct2\"\ntr.TRANSCRIPTS = WORK / \"transcripts\"\ntr.ct2_path = lambda model, quant: (original_ct2 / f\"{tr._slug(model)}__{quant}\"\n                                  if model == baseline else original_path(model, quant))\nrows = all_rows()\nrows = rows[(rows.split == \"val\") & rows.corpus.isin([\"atcosim\", \"uwb\"])]\nscores = {}\nfor name, model in models.items():\n    got = tr.transcribe_rows(model, rows, device=\"cuda\").set_index(\"uid\")\n    scores[name] = {c: ev.wer(list(f.text), [normalise(t) for t in got.loc[f.uid].hyp.fillna(\"\")])\n                    for c, f in rows.groupby(\"corpus\")}\n    print(name, scores[name], flush=True)\n    Path(__file__).with_name(\"precomputed_validation.json\").write_text(json.dumps(scores, indent=2))\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "ml_pipeline/reports/voice_tuning_20261110/precomputed_validation.json",
      "sha256": "beb1b6a679fe15dff75f427f3ed83373d4b69ab860ded6788901f1edc4f2c3a5",
      "bytes": 136,
      "encoding": "utf-8",

Synthetic speech cannot validate real Indian ATC.\",\r\n  \"sources\": [\r\n    \"https://huggingface.co/blog/fine-tune-whisper\",\r\n    \"https://research.tudelft.nl/en/publications/whisper-atc-open-models-for-air-traffic-control-automatic-speech-/\",\r\n    \"https://huggingface.co/datasets/Jzuluaga/uwb_atcc\"\r\n  ],\r\n  \"design\": \"Trial budget, learning rates and 0.5-point validation guard are our own experimental design, not a paper's guarantee.\",\r\n  \"tts_manifest_sha256\": {\r\n    \"manifest.parquet\": \"7bd3ee1f2a015c54c382f46192aac29850604948ab5a605bcbd897a384249b99\"\r\n  },\r\n  \"derived_candidates\": {\r\n    \"uniform_soup3\": \"equal full-weight average of original, continue_lr1e4 and indian_mix_lr5e5\"\r\n  },\r\n  \"amendments\": [\r\n    {\r\n      \"utc\": \"2026-11-10T09:33:24.689148+00:00\",\r\n      \"reason\": \"Full validation: real-only continuation 12.08/10.79 versus original 12.10/10.64; accent step-200 worsens recorded-speech validation while improving generated voices. Add one uniform weight average before any final test inference. Selection rule unchanged.\"\r\n    }\r\n  ]\r\n}"

    {
      "path": "ml_pipeline/reports/voice_tuning_20261110/results.md",
      "sha256": "b045e8879f9a22cade0d86a60b9a4ed42d1cf0a829743a46d82c3a5ff2507a2c",
      "bytes": 1761,
      "encoding": "utf-8",
      "content": "# Speech recognition tuning \u2014 measured results\r\n\r\nValidation-selected result: **uniform_soup3**. Lower word error is better.\r\n\r\n## Comparison used to choose the model\r\n\r\nAll 1,632 real validation recordings, same application decoder for every model.\r\n\r\n| Model | ATCOSIM word error % | UWB word error % |\r\n| --- | ---: | ---: |\r\n| baseline | 12.1 | 10.64 |\r\n| continue_lr1e4 | 12.08 | 10.79 |\r\n| indian_mix_lr5e5 | 12.67 | 10.99 |\r\n| uniform_soup3 | 11.92 | 10.41 |\r\n\r\n## Final test\r\n\r\nOnly the validation winner received new test inference.\r\n\r\n| Recording collection | Clips | Previous word error % | Selected word error % |\r\n| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: |\r\n| atco2_clean | 762 | 28.29 | 27.95 |\r\n| atco2_contaminated | 109 | 17.4 | 17.34 |\r\n| atcosim_heldout_speakers | 1712 | 4.88 | 4.8 |\r\n| uwb_heldout_recordings | 2814 | 15.11 | 15.33 |\r\n| svarah_indian_english | 6656 | 33.17 | 31.46 |\r\n\r\nComplete radio instructions correct: 64.52% \u2192 67.74% on 31 annotated instructions. This is a small sample.\r\n\r\nWord error counts words substituted, omitted or added per 100 reference words; it is not the percentage of whole sentences correct.\r\n\r\nIndian everyday English is reported separately from radio speech. Generated Indian practice voices cannot establish accuracy on real Indian controllers.\r\n\r\nThe historical test has been examined before; these results are exploratory. Only two limited-budget training trials were searched, so no global-best claim is justified.\r\n\r\nThe application's installed speech model was not changed. New exported weights, adapters and compressed runtime weights are in checkpoints/voice_20261110. The UWB training source has noncommercial share-alike terms.\r\n\r\nRecorded conflict detection remains 270/284 (95.1%), not remeasured.\r\n"
    },

    {

      "bytes": 740,
      "encoding": "utf-8",
      "content": "{\r\n  \"winner\": \"uniform_soup3\",\r\n  \"model\": \"C:\\\\Users\\\\singh\\\\OneDrive\\\\Desktop\\\\Archit\\\\KJ Somaiya\\\\LY Project\\\\Flight_Right\\\\checkpoints\\\\voice_20261110\\\\models\\\\uniform_soup3\",\r\n  \"runtime_model\": \"C:\\\\Users\\\\singh\\\\OneDrive\\\\Desktop\\\\Archit\\\\KJ Somaiya\\\\LY Project\\\\Flight_Right\\\\checkpoints\\\\voice_20261110\\\\ct2\\\\local__uniform_soup3__int8_float16\",\r\n  \"validation\": {\r\n    \"baseline\": {\r\n      \"atcosim\": 12.1,\r\n      \"uwb\": 10.64\r\n    },\r\n    \"continue_lr1e4\": {\r\n      \"atcosim\": 12.08,\r\n      \"uwb\": 10.79\r\n    },\r\n    \"indian_mix_lr5e5\": {\r\n      \"atcosim\": 12.67,\r\n      \"uwb\": 10.99\r\n    },\r\n    \"uniform_soup3\": {\r\n      \"atcosim\": 11.92,\r\n      \"uwb\": 10.41\r\n    }\r\n  },\r\n  \"frozen_utc\": \"2026-11-10T10:08:20.785274+00:00\"\r\n}"
```
