    {
      "path": "ml_pipeline/reports/voice_tuning_20261110/source_at_training/ml_pipeline/tests/test_voice_training.py",
      "sha256": "4a3758d5f8dc0f2b996889462c77ff87e05dd156d697b776f44ac5788aa02883",
      "bytes": 4421,
      "encoding": "utf-8",
      "content": "\"\"\"Checks for dropped training tails, failed producers and validation-only selection.\"\"\"\nimport unittest\nimport tempfile\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom types import SimpleNamespace\nimport pandas as pd\nimport torch\nfrom ml_pipeline.voice.blend_whisper import average_into\nfrom ml_pipeline.voice.train_whisper import accumulation_group_size, update_boundary, batches\nfrom ml_pipeline.voice.transcribe import ct2_path\nfrom scripts.experiment_voice_tuning import choose, freeze_selection\n\n\nclass VoiceTrainingTests(unittest.TestCase):\n    def test_weight_average_preserves_ties_and_unchanged_tensors(self):\n        shared = torch.zeros(2)\n        other = torch.zeros(1)\n        model = SimpleNamespace(state_dict=lambda: {\"shared\": shared, \"alias\": shared, \"other\": other})\n        readers = [SimpleNamespace(get_tensor=lambda key, n=n: {\"shared\": torch.tensor([n, n + 2.]),\n                                                               \"other\": torch.tensor([2.])}[key])\n                   for n in (1., 3., 5.)]\n        self.assertEqual(average_into(model, readers), (1, 1))\n        torch.testing.assert_close(shared, torch.tensor([3., 5.]))\n        self.assertTrue(torch.equal(other, torch.tensor([2.])))\n\n    def test_unaligned_average_is_rejected(self):\n        model = SimpleNamespace(state_dict=lambda: {\"weight\": torch.zeros(2)})\n        readers = [SimpleNamespace(get_tensor=lambda key: torch.zeros(3))]\n        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, \"Unaligned source tensor\"):\n            average_into(model, readers)\n\n    def test_selection_timestamp_and_winner_remain_frozen(self):\n        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:\n            path = Path(tmp) / \"selection.json\"\n            original = dict(winner=\"baseline\", model=\"model\", validation={\"baseline\": {\"a\": 10}},\n                            frozen_utc=\"initial\")\n            freeze_selection(path, original)\n            self.assertEqual(freeze_selection(path, dict(original, frozen_utc=\"later\")), original)\n            with self.assertRaisesRegex(RuntimeError, \"Frozen validation choice changed\"):\n                freeze_selection(path, dict(original, winner=\"other\"))\n\n    def test_ready_runtime_model_does_not_need_reconversion(self):\n        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:\n            path = Path(tmp)\n            for name in (\"model.bin\", \"config.json\", \"tokenizer.json\", \"preprocessor_config.json\"):\n                (path / name).touch()\n            self.assertEqual(ct2_path(str(path), \"int8_float16\"), path.resolve())\n\n    def test_incomplete_runtime_model_is_rejected(self):\n        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:\n            (Path(tmp) / \"model.bin\").touch()\n            with self.assertRaisesRegex(RuntimeError, \"Incomplete runtime model\"):\n                ct2_path(tmp, \"int8_float16\")\n\n    def test_tail_has_correct_loss_scale_and_is_updated(self):\n        for count in range(1, 12):\n            for accumulation in range(1, 5):\n                sizes = [accumulation_group_size(i, count, accumulation) for i in range(count)]\n                self.assertAlmostEqual(sum(1 / n for n in sizes),\n                                       (count + accumulation - 1) // accumulation)\n                self.assertEqual(sizes[-1], (count - 1) % accumulation + 1)\n                updates = [i for i in range(count) if update_boundary(i, count, accumulation)]\n                self.assertEqual(len(updates), (count + accumulation - 1) // accumulation)\n                self.assertEqual(updates[-1], count - 1)\n\n    def test_audio_failure_reaches_consumer(self):\n        def bad_audio(row):\n            raise ValueError(\"damaged recording\")\n        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, \"damaged recording\"):\n            list(batches(pd.DataFrame({\"text\": [\"hello\"]}), [0], bad_audio,\n                         SimpleNamespace(feature_extractor=lambda *a, **k: None), 1))\n\n    def test_winner_cannot_trade_large_corpus_regression_for_mean(self):\n        scores = {\"baseline\": {\"atcosim\": 10, \"uwb\": 20},\n                  \"bad_trade\": {\"atcosim\": 11, \"uwb\": 10},\n                  \"better\": {\"atcosim\": 9, \"uwb\": 19}}\n        self.assertEqual(choose(scores), \"better\")\n\n    def test_baseline_retained_when_candidates_are_worse(self):\n        self.assertEqual(choose({\"baseline\": {\"a\": 5, \"b\": 10},\n                                 \"candidate\": {\"a\": 6, \"b\": 11}}), \"baseline\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    unittest.main()\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "ml_pipeline/reports/voice_tuning_20261110/source_at_training/ml_pipeline/voice/blend_whisper.py",
      "sha256": "c293025fbfd4bc673d745be33cb37eddfc4e7f11b1e7372148fa616cd4a4dc04",
      "bytes": 3710,
      "encoding": "utf-8",
      "content": "\"\"\"Uniform full-weight average of aligned fine-tunes; validation must justify use.\"\"\"\nfrom contextlib import ExitStack\nimport argparse\nimport hashlib\nimport json\nfrom pathlib import Path\nimport sys\n\nROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]\nsys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))\n\n\ndef average_into(model, readers):\n    import torch\n    preserved = changed = 0\n    pointers = set()\n    with torch.no_grad():\n        for key, destination in model.state_dict().items():\n            if destination.data_ptr() in pointers:\n                continue\n            pointers.add(destination.data_ptr())\n            tensors = [r.get_tensor(key) for r in readers]\n            if any(t.shape != destination.shape for t in tensors):\n                raise ValueError(f\"Unaligned source tensor: {key}\")\n            if all(torch.equal(tensors[0], t) for t in tensors[1:]):\n                destination.copy_(tensors[0])\n                preserved += 1\n            else:\n                average = torch.zeros_like(destination)\n                for tensor in tensors:\n                    average.add_(tensor, alpha=1 / len(tensors))\n                destination.copy_(average)\n                changed += 1\n    return preserved, changed\n\n\ndef main():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n    parser.add_argument(\"--sources\", nargs=\"+\", type=Path, required=True)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--output\", type=Path, required=True)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--ct2-root\", type=Path, required=True)\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n    if args.output.exists():\n        raise SystemExit(f\"Refusing to overwrite {args.output}\")\n    import torch\n    from safetensors import safe_open\n    from transformers import WhisperForConditionalGeneration, WhisperProcessor\n    from ml_pipeline.voice import transcribe\n    source_hashes = [hashlib.sha256((p / \"model.safetensors\").read_bytes()).hexdigest() for p in args.sources]\n    model = WhisperForConditionalGeneration.from_pretrained(str(args.sources[0]))\n    # Own writable storage, even if the loader used memory-mapped source tensors.\n    for tensor in list(model.parameters()) + list(model.buffers()):\n        tensor.data = tensor.data.clone()\n    with ExitStack() as stack, torch.no_grad():\n        readers = [stack.enter_context(safe_open(str(p / \"model.safetensors\"), framework=\"pt\", device=\"cpu\"))\n                   for p in args.sources]\n        preserved, changed = average_into(model, readers)\n    model.generation_config.forced_decoder_ids = None\n    model.save_pretrained(args.output)\n    processor = WhisperProcessor.from_pretrained(str(args.sources[0]))\n    processor.save_pretrained(args.output)\n    processor.feature_extractor.save_pretrained(args.output)\n    provenance = {\"method\": \"uniform full-weight average; unchanged tensors preserved exactly\",\n                  \"sources\": [{\"path\": str(p.resolve()), \"weight\": 1 / len(args.sources), \"sha256\": digest}\n                              for p, digest in zip(args.sources, source_hashes)],\n                  \"unchanged_tensors\": preserved, \"averaged_tensors\": changed,\n                  \"source\": \"https://proceedings.mlr.press/v162/wortsman22a.html\",\n                  \"scope\": \"Paper is not evidence of ASR improvement; our validation determines eligibility\"}\n    (args.output / \"flightright_provenance.json\").write_text(json.dumps(provenance, indent=2))\n    for path, digest in zip(args.sources, source_hashes):\n        if hashlib.sha256((path / \"model.safetensors\").read_bytes()).hexdigest() != digest:\n            raise RuntimeError(f\"Source weights changed: {path}\")\n    transcribe.CT2_DIR = args.ct2_root\n    print(\"runtime:\", transcribe.ct2_path(str(args.output), \"int8_float16\"), flush=True)\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "ml_pipeline/reports/voice_tuning_20261110/source_at_training/ml_pipeline/voice/export_whisper.py",
      "sha256": "9c24b13fbd55bb67f0ae7b953ee8c69ce79b37183c3ed32c99a46f0b33a7bcc6",
      "bytes": 2672,
      "encoding": "utf-8",
      "content": "\"\"\"Turn a LoRA adapter into an ordinary Whisper checkpoint that any code path can load.\r\n\r\n    python ml_pipeline/voice/export_whisper.py --adapter <run dir>/best_adapter --name flightright-whisper-small-atc-p1\r\n\r\nMerges the adapter into the base weights (peft ``merge_and_unload``), saves model and processor\r\nin Hugging Face format under <voice cache>/models/<name>, and converts that to CTranslate2 int8.\r\nAfter this, our own model is named exactly like a published one -- by setting\r\nFLIGHT_RIGHT_ASR_MODEL_ID to the directory -- and nothing else changes (plan 4.3 / 9.3).\r\n\"\"\"\r\nfrom __future__ import annotations\r\n\r\nimport argparse\r\nimport json\r\nimport sys\r\nfrom pathlib import Path\r\n\r\nROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]\r\nsys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))\r\nfrom ml_pipeline.voice.build_audio_cache import CACHE  # noqa: E402\r\n\r\n\r\ndef main():\r\n    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()\r\n    ap.add_argument(\"--adapter\", required=True)\r\n    ap.add_argument(\"--name\", required=True)\r\n    ap.add_argument(\"--base\", default=None, help=\"defaults to the base recorded in the adapter config\")\r\n    ap.add_argument(\"--output-root\", type=Path, default=CACHE / \"models\")\r\n    ap.add_argument(\"--ct2-root\", type=Path, default=None)\r\n    args = ap.parse_args()\r\n    from peft import PeftConfig, PeftModel\r\n    from transformers import WhisperForConditionalGeneration, WhisperProcessor\r\n    base = args.base or PeftConfig.from_pretrained(args.adapter).base_model_name_or_path\r\n    model = WhisperForConditionalGeneration.from_pretrained(base)\r\n    model = PeftModel.from_pretrained(model, args.adapter).merge_and_unload()\r\n    model.generation_config.forced_decoder_ids = None\r\n    out = args.output_root / args.name\r\n    if out.exists():\r\n        raise SystemExit(f\"Refusing to overwrite existing export: {out}\")\r\n    model.save_pretrained(out)\r\n    processor = WhisperProcessor.from_pretrained(base)\r\n    processor.save_pretrained(out)\r\n    # transformers 5 writes the audio settings as processor_config.json; faster-whisper and the\r\n    # CTranslate2 converter look for preprocessor_config.json, so write that name too.\r\n    processor.feature_extractor.save_pretrained(out)\r\n    (out / \"flightright_provenance.json\").write_text(json.dumps(\r\n        {\"base_model\": base, \"adapter\": str(Path(args.adapter).resolve()),\r\n         \"train_state\": str(Path(args.adapter).resolve().parent / \"state.json\")}, indent=2))\r\n    from ml_pipeline.voice import transcribe\r\n    if args.ct2_root is not None:\r\n        transcribe.CT2_DIR = args.ct2_root\r\n    print(\"merged:\", out)\r\n    print(\"ctranslate2:\", transcribe.ct2_path(str(out), \"int8_float16\"))\r\n\r\n\r\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\r\n    main()\r\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "ml_pipeline/reports/voice_tuning_20261110/source_at_training/ml_pipeline/voice/train_whisper.py",
      "sha256": "62df9d14c7b8077961d45e30cc22683ee5c28d3c53d537b5a6d2dbbfe81c725d",
      "bytes": 17710,
      "encoding": "utf-8",
      "content": "\"\"\"Fine-tune Whisper on air-traffic speech with LoRA (plan 9.3 / revised speech plan, step 5).\r\n\r\n    python ml_pipeline/voice/train_whisper.py --stage p1 --run radio_run\r\n    python ml_pipeline/voice/train_whisper.py --stage p2 --init <p1 adapter dir> --run accent_run\r\n    python ml_pipeline/voice/train_whisper.py --stage p1 --run radio_run --resume\r\n\r\nReads the cache and split written by build_audio_cache.py; never decides a split itself.\r\n\r\nWHY LoRA ON whisper-small. The laptop GPU has 6 GB. Measured on 25 Sep: LoRA (r=32 on every\r\nattention and feed-forward projection, 13.0 M trainable of 254.7 M) with gradient\r\ncheckpointing runs at 1.58 s per batch of 8 in 2.64 GB; without checkpointing it needed\r\n8.85 GB, spilled into shared system memory and took 15.5 s per batch. whisper-small is\r\nthe model plan 9.3 names; the published number to beat is 15.1 % WER.\r\n\r\nWHAT IS PRINTED BESIDE WHAT (plan rule 2). The starting checkpoint is scored on the same fixed\r\nvalidation subset before the first step, so every later figure has its baseline next to it.\r\nValidation WER is reported per corpus, never averaged into one number (rule 4); the\r\ncheckpoint kept is the one with the lowest MEAN of the real per-corpus WERs, so the larger corpus\r\ncannot outvote the other.\r\n\r\nTest data is never read here. Scoring on test is evaluate_voice.py's job, once, at the end.\r\n\"\"\"\r\nfrom __future__ import annotations\r\n\r\nimport argparse\r\nimport json\r\nimport math\r\nimport os\r\nimport queue\r\nimport random\r\nimport sys\r\nimport threading\r\nimport time\r\nfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\r\nfrom pathlib import Path\r\n\r\nimport jiwer\r\nimport numpy as np\r\nimport pandas as pd\r\nimport torch\r\n\r\nROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]\r\nsys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))\r\nfrom ml_pipeline.voice.build_audio_cache import CACHE, SR  # noqa: E402\r\nfrom ml_pipeline.voice.text_norm import normalise  # noqa: E402\r\n\r\nREPORTS = ROOT / \"ml_pipeline/reports\"\r\nBASE_MODEL = os.environ.get(\"FLIGHT_RIGHT_ASR_BASE\", \"openai/whisper-small\")\r\nSTAGES = {\r\n    # corpus -> weight. p2 keeps European audio in the mix so the accent pass cannot make the\r\n    # model forget real radio (catastrophic forgetting is the standard failure of a second\r\n    # fine-tuning pass on a narrow set).\r\n    \"p1\": {\"atcosim\": 1.0, \"uwb\": 1.0},\r\n    \"p2\": {\"tts_indian\": 1.0, \"atcosim\": 0.25, \"uwb\": 0.25},\r\n}\r\nVAL_PER_CORPUS = int(os.environ.get(\"FLIGHT_RIGHT_VOICE_VAL_PER_CORPUS\", 200))\r\nSEED = 42\r\n\r\n\r\ndef parse_args():\r\n    p = argparse.ArgumentParser()\r\n    p.add_argument(\"--stage\", choices=sorted(STAGES), default=\"p1\")\r\n    p.add_argument(\"--init\", help=\"adapter directory to start from (p2 starts from p1's best)\")\r\n    p.add_argument(\"--resume\", action=\"store_true\")\r\n    p.add_argument(\"--epochs\", type=float, default=3.0)\r\n    p.add_argument(\"--batch\", type=int, default=8)\r\n    p.add_argument(\"--accum\", type=int, default=2)\r\n    p.add_argument(\"--lr\", type=float, default=5e-4)\r\n    p.add_argument(\"--warmup\", type=int, default=300)\r\n    p.add_argument(\"--eval-every\", type=int, default=500, help=\"optimizer steps\")\r\n    p.add_argument(\"--patience\", type=int, default=3)\r\n    p.add_argument(\"--rank\", type=int, default=32)\r\n    p.add_argument(\"--run\", default=None)\r\n    p.add_argument(\"--output-root\", type=Path, default=CACHE / \"runs\")\r\n    p.add_argument(\"--max-steps\", type=int, default=None)\r\n    p.add_argument(\"--val-per-corpus\", type=int, default=VAL_PER_CORPUS)\r\n    return p.parse_args()\r\n\r\n\r\nclass Audio:\r\n    \"\"\"Random access into the int16 caches without loading gigabytes into memory.\r\n\r\n    Real corpora live in audio_int16.bin; generated speech in tts/audio_int16.bin. Each row's\r\n    ``bin`` column says which (missing = the real-corpus file).\"\"\"\r\n\r\n    def __init__(self):\r\n        self.files: dict[str, np.memmap] = {}\r\n\r\n    def __call__(self, row) -> np.ndarray:\r\n        name = getattr(row, \"bin\", None)\r\n        name = name if isinstance(name, str) and name else \"audio_int16.bin\"\r\n        if name not in self.files:\r\n            self.files[name] = np.memmap(CACHE / name, dtype=np.int16, mode=\"r\")\r\n        data = self.files[name]\r\n        return data[int(row.offset): int(row.offset) + int(row.n_samples)].astype(np.float32) / 32768.0\r\n\r\n\r\ndef all_rows() -> pd.DataFrame:\r\n    \"\"\"The real-corpus manifest plus, when it exists, the generated-speech manifest.\"\"\"\r\n    frames = [pd.read_parquet(CACHE / \"manifest.parquet\")]\r\n    # One manifest per synthesis shard (synthesize.py --shard i/n, e.g. one per GPU); each row\r\n    # names its own audio file in \"bin\".\r\n    for path in sorted((CACHE / \"tts\").glob(\"manifest*.parquet\")):\r\n        frames.append(pd.read_parquet(path))\r\n    return pd.concat(frames, ignore_index=True)\r\n\r\n\r\ndef load_rows(stage: str, split: str) -> pd.DataFrame:\r\n    manifest = all_rows()\r\n    rows = manifest[(manifest.split == split) & manifest.corpus.isin(STAGES[stage])]\r\n    missing = set(STAGES[stage]) - set(rows.corpus)\r\n    if missing:\r\n        raise SystemExit(f\"stage {stage} needs corpora not in the cache: {sorted(missing)}\")\r\n    return rows.reset_index(drop=True)\r\n\r\n\r\ndef epoch_order(rows: pd.DataFrame, stage: str, rng: random.Random) -> list[int]:\r\n    \"\"\"Indices for one epoch, with each corpus subsampled to its stage weight.\"\"\"\r\n    order = []\r\n    for corpus, weight in STAGES[stage].items():\r\n        idx = rows.index[rows.corpus == corpus].tolist()\r\n        rng.shuffle(idx)\r\n        order += idx[: max(1, int(round(len(idx) * weight)))]\r\n    rng.shuffle(order)\r\n    return order\r\n\r\n\r\ndef label_ids(tokenizer, text: str) -> list[int]:\r\n    ids = tokenizer(text).input_ids\r\n    # The model prepends decoder_start_token_id itself when it shifts labels right.\r\n    if ids and ids[0] == tokenizer.convert_tokens_to_ids(\"<|startoftranscript|>\"):\r\n        ids = ids[1:]\r\n    return ids[:440]\r\n\r\n\r\ndef accumulation_group_size(batch_index: int, batch_count: int, accumulation: int) -> int:\r\n    \"\"\"Number of microbatches contributing to this optimizer update, including the tail.\"\"\"\r\n    return min(accumulation, batch_count - (batch_index // accumulation) * accumulation)\r\n\r\n\r\ndef update_boundary(batch_index: int, batch_count: int, accumulation: int) -> bool:\r\n    return (batch_index + 1) % accumulation == 0 or batch_index + 1 == batch_count\r\n\r\n\r\ndef batches(rows, order, audio, processor, batch_size):\r\n    \"\"\"Feature extraction on a background thread so the GPU never waits on the CPU.\"\"\"\r\n    q: queue.Queue = queue.Queue(maxsize=6)\r\n\r\n    def produce():\r\n        for start in range(0, len(order), batch_size):\r\n            chunk = rows.loc[order[start: start + batch_size]]\r\n            feats = processor.feature_extractor([audio(r) for r in chunk.itertuples()],\r\n                                                sampling_rate=SR, return_tensors=\"np\").input_features\r\n            labels = [label_ids(processor.tokenizer, t) for t in chunk.text]\r\n            width = max(len(x) for x in labels)\r\n            lab = np.full((len(labels), width), -100, dtype=np.int64)\r\n            for i, x in enumerate(labels):\r\n                lab[i, : len(x)] = x\r\n            q.put((torch.from_numpy(feats), torch.from_numpy(lab)))\r\n\r\n    def work():\r\n        try:\r\n            produce()\r\n        except Exception as exc:\r\n            q.put(exc)\r\n        finally:\r\n            q.put(None)\r\n\r\n    threading.Thread(target=work, daemon=True).start()\r\n    while (item := q.get()) is not None:\r\n        if isinstance(item, Exception):\r\n            raise item\r\n        yield item\r\n\r\n\r\n@torch.no_grad()\r\ndef evaluate(model, processor, rows, audio) -> dict:\r\n    model.eval()\r\n    out = {}\r\n    for corpus, frame in rows.groupby(\"corpus\"):\r\n        hyps = []\r\n        for start in range(0, len(frame), 16):\r\n            chunk = frame.iloc[start: start + 16]\r\n            feats = processor.feature_extractor([audio(r) for r in chunk.itertuples()],\r\n                                                sampling_rate=SR, return_tensors=\"pt\").input_features\r\n            with torch.autocast(\"cuda\", dtype=torch.float16):\r\n                ids = model.generate(input_features=feats.cuda(), language=\"en\", task=\"transcribe\",\r\n                                     max_new_tokens=160, use_cache=True)\r\n            hyps += processor.tokenizer.batch_decode(ids, skip_special_tokens=True)\r\n        refs = list(frame.text)\r\n        hyps = [normalise(h) for h in hyps]\r\n        pairs = [(r, h) for r, h in zip(refs, hyps) if r]\r\n        out[corpus] = round(100 * jiwer.wer([r for r, _ in pairs], [h for _, h in pairs]), 2)\r\n    model.train()\r\n    # Synthetic voices are a diagnostic, not evidence of real-radio improvement.\r\n    out[\"mean\"] = round(float(np.mean([v for k, v in out.items() if k != \"tts_indian\"])), 2)\r\n    return out\r\n\r\n\r\ndef main():\r\n    args = parse_args()\r\n    if args.resume and not args.run:\r\n        raise SystemExit(\"--resume requires --run to identify the saved run\")\r\n    from peft import LoraConfig, PeftModel, get_peft_model\r\n    from transformers import WhisperForConditionalGeneration, WhisperProcessor\r\n\r\n    torch.manual_seed(SEED)\r\n    run = args.run or f\"{args.stage}_{datetime.now():%Y%m%d_%H%M%S}\"\r\n    run_dir = args.output_root / run\r\n    if run_dir.exists() and not args.resume:\r\n        raise SystemExit(f\"Refusing to overwrite existing run: {run_dir}\")\r\n    run_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\r\n    state_path = run_dir / \"state.json\"\r\n    if args.resume and not (state_path.exists() and (run_dir / \"last_adapter\").exists()):\r\n        raise SystemExit(f\"No complete resumable checkpoint in {run_dir}\")\r\n\r\n    processor = WhisperProcessor.from_pretrained(BASE_MODEL)\r\n    processor.tokenizer.set_prefix_tokens(language=\"english\", task=\"transcribe\")\r\n    audio = Audio()\r\n    train = load_rows(args.stage, \"train\")\r\n    val_all = load_rows(args.stage, \"val\")\r\n    val = pd.concat([f.sample(min(len(f), args.val_per_corpus), random_state=SEED)\r\n                     for _, f in val_all.groupby(\"corpus\")]).reset_index(drop=True)\r\n\r\n    model = WhisperForConditionalGeneration.from_pretrained(BASE_MODEL)\r\n    model.config.use_cache = False\r\n    model.generation_config.forced_decoder_ids = None\r\n    model.gradient_checkpointing_enable(gradient_checkpointing_kwargs={\"use_reentrant\": False})\r\n    model.enable_input_require_grads()\r\n    resume = args.resume and (run_dir / \"last_adapter\").exists()\r\n    start_from = run_dir / \"last_adapter\" if resume else (Path(args.init) if args.init else None)\r\n    if start_from:\r\n        model = PeftModel.from_pretrained(model, str(start_from), is_trainable=True)\r\n    else:\r\n        model = get_peft_model(model, LoraConfig(\r\n            r=args.rank, lora_alpha=2 * args.rank, lora_dropout=0.05,\r\n            target_modules=[\"q_proj\", \"k_proj\", \"v_proj\", \"out_proj\", \"fc1\", \"fc2\"]))\r\n    model.cuda()\r\n    trainable = sum(p.numel() for p in model.parameters() if p.requires_grad)\r\n\r\n    epoch_indices = epoch_order(train, args.stage, random.Random(0))\r\n    steps_per_epoch = math.ceil(len(epoch_indices) / (args.batch * args.accum))\r\n    total_steps = int(steps_per_epoch * args.epochs)\r\n    if args.max_steps is not None:\r\n        total_steps = min(total_steps, args.max_steps)\r\n    if min(args.batch, args.accum, args.warmup, args.eval_every, total_steps) < 1:\r\n        raise SystemExit(\"Batch, accumulation, warmup, evaluation interval and steps must be positive\")\r\n    opt = torch.optim.AdamW([p for p in model.parameters() if p.requires_grad], lr=args.lr, weight_decay=0.01)\r\n    sched = torch.optim.lr_scheduler.LambdaLR(\r\n        opt, lambda s: min(1.0, (s + 1) / args.warmup) * max(0.0, (total_steps - s) / max(1, total_steps - args.warmup)))\r\n    scaler = torch.amp.GradScaler()\r\n\r\n    state = {\"run\": run, \"stage\": args.stage, \"base_model\": BASE_MODEL, \"init\": args.init,\r\n             \"args\": {k: str(v) if isinstance(v, Path) else v for k, v in vars(args).items()}, \"trainable_params\": trainable,\r\n             \"train_clips\": train.corpus.value_counts().to_dict(),\r\n             \"weighted_epoch_clips\": train.loc[epoch_indices].corpus.value_counts().to_dict(),\r\n             \"val_clips_scored\": val.corpus.value_counts().to_dict(),\r\n             \"steps_per_epoch\": steps_per_epoch, \"total_steps\": total_steps,\r\n             \"step\": 0, \"epoch\": 0, \"epoch_clips\": 0, \"evals\": [], \"best\": None, \"stale\": 0, \"started_utc\": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()}\r\n    if resume and state_path.exists():\r\n        saved = json.loads(state_path.read_text())\r\n        if saved[\"base_model\"] != BASE_MODEL:\r\n            raise SystemExit(\"Cannot resume with a different base model\")\r\n        for key in (\"stage\", \"batch\", \"accum\", \"lr\", \"warmup\", \"epochs\", \"max_steps\", \"val_per_corpus\"):\r\n            if saved[\"args\"].get(key) != state[\"args\"].get(key):\r\n                raise SystemExit(f\"Cannot resume with changed setting: {key}; start a new continuation instead\")\r\n        state = saved\r\n        opt.load_state_dict(torch.load(run_dir / \"last_optimizer.pt\"))\r\n        sched.load_state_dict(torch.load(run_dir / \"last_scheduler.pt\"))\r\n        if \"epoch_clips\" not in state:\r\n            raise SystemExit(\"Legacy run lacks an exact data cursor; use --init for a new continuation run\")\r\n        scaler.load_state_dict(torch.load(run_dir / \"last_scaler.pt\"))\r\n        torch.set_rng_state(torch.load(run_dir / \"last_rng.pt\")[\"cpu\"])\r\n        torch.cuda.set_rng_state_all(torch.load(run_dir / \"last_rng.pt\")[\"cuda\"])\r\n        print(f\"resumed {run} at optimizer step {state['step']}\")\r\n    print(f\"run {run}: {len(train):,} train clips {state['train_clips']}, {trainable/1e6:.1f} M trainable, \"\r\n          f\"{steps_per_epoch} optimizer steps per epoch, {total_steps} total\", flush=True)\r\n\r\n    def checkpoint(tag: str):\r\n        model.save_pretrained(run_dir / tag)\r\n        if tag == \"last_adapter\":\r\n            torch.save(opt.state_dict(), run_dir / \"last_optimizer.pt\")\r\n            torch.save(sched.state_dict(), run_dir / \"last_scheduler.pt\")\r\n            torch.save(scaler.state_dict(), run_dir / \"last_scaler.pt\")\r\n            torch.save({\"cpu\": torch.get_rng_state(), \"cuda\": torch.cuda.get_rng_state_all()}, run_dir / \"last_rng.pt\")\r\n\r\n    def record_eval():\r\n        t0 = time.time()\r\n        wer = evaluate(model, processor, val, audio)\r\n        entry = {\"step\": state[\"step\"], \"wer\": wer, \"seconds\": round(time.time() - t0, 1),\r\n                 \"utc\": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()}\r\n        state[\"evals\"].append(entry)\r\n        improved = state[\"best\"] is None or wer[\"mean\"] < state[\"best\"][\"wer\"][\"mean\"]\r\n        if improved:\r\n            state[\"best\"], state[\"stale\"] = entry, 0\r\n            checkpoint(\"best_adapter\")\r\n        elif state[\"step\"] > 0:\r\n            state[\"stale\"] += 1\r\n        checkpoint(\"last_adapter\")\r\n        state_path.write_text(json.dumps(state, indent=2))\r\n        base = state[\"evals\"][0][\"wer\"]\r\n        print(f\"[eval] step {state['step']:5d}  WER {wer}  (starting checkpoint: {base})\"\r\n              f\"{'  <- best' if improved and state['step'] else ''}\", flush=True)\r\n\r\n    if not state[\"evals\"]:\r\n        record_eval()  # the starting model's baseline on the same subset\r\n\r\n    model.train()\r\n    epoch = state[\"epoch\"]\r\n    t_last = time.time()\r\n    while state[\"step\"] < total_steps and state[\"stale\"] < args.patience:\r\n        order = epoch_order(train, args.stage, random.Random(SEED + epoch))\r\n        skip = state[\"epoch_clips\"]\r\n        losses = []\r\n        remaining_batches = math.ceil((len(order) - skip) / args.batch)\r\n        for batch_index, (feats, lab) in enumerate(batches(train, order[skip:], audio, processor, args.batch)):\r\n            # The final, shorter accumulation group must also produce an update.\r\n            group_size = accumulation_group_size(batch_index, remaining_batches, args.accum)\r\n            with torch.autocast(\"cuda\", dtype=torch.float16):\r\n                loss = model(input_features=feats.cuda(), labels=lab.cuda()).loss / group_size\r\n            scaler.scale(loss).backward()\r\n            losses.append(loss.item() * group_size)\r\n            state[\"epoch_clips\"] += len(lab)\r\n            if not update_boundary(batch_index, remaining_batches, args.accum):\r\n                continue\r\n            scaler.unscale_(opt)\r\n            torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_([p for p in model.parameters() if p.requires_grad], 1.0)\r\n            scaler.step(opt); scaler.update(); opt.zero_grad(set_to_none=True); sched.step()\r\n            state[\"step\"] += 1\r\n            if state[\"step\"] % 50 == 0:\r\n                rate = (time.time() - t_last) / 50\r\n                t_last = time.time()\r\n                print(f\"step {state['step']:5d}/{total_steps}  loss {np.mean(losses[-100:]):.3f}  \"\r\n                      f\"lr {sched.get_last_lr()[0]:.2e}  {rate:.2f} s/step  \"\r\n                      f\"eta {(total_steps - state['step']) * rate / 3600:.1f} h\", flush=True)\r\n            if state[\"step\"] % args.eval_every == 0:\r\n                record_eval()\r\n                if state[\"stale\"] >= args.patience:\r\n                    print(\"validation WER stopped improving; stopping early\")\r\n                    break\r\n            if state[\"step\"] >= total_steps:\r\n                break\r\n        epoch += 1\r\n        if state[\"epoch_clips\"] == len(order):\r\n            state[\"epoch\"], state[\"epoch_clips\"] = epoch, 0\r\n\r\n    if state[\"evals\"][-1][\"step\"] != state[\"step\"]:\r\n        record_eval()\r\n    state[\"finished_utc\"] = datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()\r\n    state_path.write_text(json.dumps(state, indent=2))\r\n    REPORTS.mkdir(exist_ok=True)\r\n    (REPORTS / f\"voice_train_{run}.json\").write_text(json.dumps(state, indent=2))\r\n    print(f\"done. best {state['best']}\\nadapter: {run_dir / 'best_adapter'}\")\r\n\r\n\r\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\r\n    main()\r\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "ml_pipeline/reports/voice_tuning_20261110/source_at_training/ml_pipeline/voice/transcribe.py",
      "sha256": "26a48b4a6425968325671d5089c0d2f45bbea4873d2e3c7fe41e4f18e889967e",
      "bytes": 8325,
      "encoding": "utf-8",
      "content": "\"\"\"Speech to text, one code path for every model (plan 4.1-4.4).\r\n\r\n    python ml_pipeline/voice/transcribe.py --model jlvdoorn/whisper-medium-atcosim --split test\r\n\r\nA model is named by its Hugging Face id, or by a local directory holding a Hugging Face\r\nWhisper checkpoint (our own fine-tune, merged). Either way it is converted ONCE to\r\nCTranslate2 and served by faster-whisper, which is what fits Whisper large into 6 GB\r\n(plan 4.2: int8 = 2,926 MB). Nothing else in the project names a model: the backend reads\r\nASR_MODEL_ID and ASR_QUANTISATION from server/app/config.py and calls load_model().\r\n\r\nTranscripts are cached per (model, quantisation) under the voice cache, so each model is run\r\nover each clip once and every later measurement reads the cache. Every row carries the\r\nmodel id, the quantisation and the decoding settings (plan 4.3: model version on every row).\r\n\r\nDecoding settings are fixed and identical for every model: English, transcribe, beam 5, no\r\ntimestamps, no conditioning on previous text, no voice-activity filter (the clips are\r\nalready segmented). Changing one would make the comparison unfair, so they are constants.\r\n\"\"\"\r\nfrom __future__ import annotations\r\n\r\nimport argparse\r\nimport os\r\nimport subprocess\r\nimport sys\r\nimport time\r\nfrom pathlib import Path\r\n\r\nimport numpy as np\r\nimport pandas as pd\r\n\r\nROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]\r\nsys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))\r\nfrom ml_pipeline.voice.build_audio_cache import CACHE, SR  # noqa: E402\r\n\r\nCT2_DIR = CACHE / \"ct2\"\r\nTRANSCRIPTS = CACHE / \"transcripts\"\r\nDECODING = dict(language=\"en\", task=\"transcribe\", beam_size=5, without_timestamps=True,\r\n                condition_on_previous_text=False, vad_filter=False, temperature=0.0)\r\nASR_VERSION = \"fw1-beam5-v1\"   # bump if DECODING changes, so cached rows cannot be mixed\r\n\r\n\r\ndef _slug(model_id: str) -> str:\r\n    \"\"\"A filesystem-safe name for a model id or a local model directory.\"\"\"\r\n    path = Path(model_id)\r\n    if path.exists():\r\n        return \"local__\" + path.resolve().name\r\n    return model_id.replace(\"/\", \"__\")\r\n\r\n\r\ndef ct2_path(model_id: str, quantisation: str) -> Path:\r\n    \"\"\"Convert a Hugging Face Whisper checkpoint to CTranslate2, once.\"\"\"\r\n    supplied = Path(model_id)\r\n    if (supplied / \"model.bin\").is_file():\r\n        required = (\"config.json\", \"tokenizer.json\", \"preprocessor_config.json\")\r\n        missing = [name for name in required if not (supplied / name).is_file()]\r\n        if missing:\r\n            raise RuntimeError(f\"Incomplete runtime model at {supplied}: missing {missing}\")\r\n        return supplied.resolve()\r\n    out = CT2_DIR / f\"{_slug(model_id)}__{quantisation}\"\r\n    if (out / \"model.bin\").exists():\r\n        return out\r\n    out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\r\n    converter = Path(sys.executable).with_name(\"ct2-transformers-converter\")\r\n    wanted = [\"tokenizer.json\", \"preprocessor_config.json\"]\r\n    if Path(model_id).exists():   # a local checkpoint: copy only what is actually there\r\n        wanted = [f for f in wanted if (Path(model_id) / f).exists()]\r\n    command = [str(converter), \"--model\", model_id, \"--output_dir\", str(out),\r\n               \"--quantization\", quantisation, \"--copy_files\", *wanted]\r\n    print(\"converting:\", \" \".join(command), flush=True)\r\n    result = subprocess.run(command, capture_output=True, text=True, encoding=\"utf-8\", errors=\"replace\")\r\n    if result.returncode != 0:\r\n        # Some checkpoints ship tokenizer files under other names; retry without copying.\r\n        command = command[: command.index(\"--copy_files\")]\r\n        result = subprocess.run(command, capture_output=True, text=True, encoding=\"utf-8\", errors=\"replace\")\r\n        if result.returncode != 0:\r\n            raise RuntimeError(f\"conversion failed for {model_id}: {result.stderr[-2000:]}\")\r\n    missing = [f for f in (\"tokenizer.json\", \"preprocessor_config.json\") if not (out / f).exists()]\r\n    if missing:\r\n        # Without these faster-whisper silently falls back to a stock tokenizer and 80 mel bins.\r\n        raise RuntimeError(f\"converted {model_id} but {missing} were not copied into {out}\")\r\n    return out\r\n\r\n\r\ndef _cuda_libraries_on_path() -> None:\r\n    \"\"\"CTranslate2 needs cuBLAS 12 and cuDNN 9 at run time and looks for them on the DLL path.\r\n\r\n    PyTorch's CUDA 12 wheel already ships both (torch/lib/cublas64_12.dll, cudnn64_9.dll), so\r\n    point Windows at that folder instead of installing a second copy. Measured 26 Sep: without\r\n    this, the first GPU transcription fails with \"cublas64_12.dll is not found\".\"\"\"\r\n    if sys.platform != \"win32\":\r\n        return\r\n    import torch\r\n    lib = Path(torch.__file__).parent / \"lib\"\r\n    if lib.exists() and str(lib) not in os.environ.get(\"PATH\", \"\"):\r\n        os.add_dll_directory(str(lib))\r\n        os.environ[\"PATH\"] = str(lib) + os.pathsep + os.environ.get(\"PATH\", \"\")\r\n\r\n\r\ndef load_model(model_id: str, quantisation: str = \"int8_float16\", device: str = \"auto\"):\r\n    from faster_whisper import WhisperModel\r\n    path = ct2_path(model_id, quantisation)\r\n    if device == \"auto\":\r\n        import ctranslate2\r\n        device = \"cuda\" if ctranslate2.get_cuda_device_count() > 0 else \"cpu\"\r\n    if device == \"cuda\":\r\n        _cuda_libraries_on_path()\r\n    compute = quantisation if device == \"cuda\" else quantisation.replace(\"_float16\", \"\")\r\n    return WhisperModel(str(path), device=device, compute_type=compute)\r\n\r\n\r\ndef transcribe_array(model, audio: np.ndarray) -> dict:\r\n    \"\"\"One clip -> text plus the recogniser's own confidence (mean token log-probability).\"\"\"\r\n    segments, _ = model.transcribe(audio.astype(np.float32), **DECODING)\r\n    segments = list(segments)\r\n    text = \" \".join(s.text.strip() for s in segments).strip()\r\n    logprob = float(np.mean([s.avg_logprob for s in segments])) if segments else float(\"nan\")\r\n    return {\"hyp\": text, \"avg_logprob\": logprob}\r\n\r\n\r\ndef transcribe_rows(model_id: str, rows: pd.DataFrame, quantisation: str = \"int8_float16\",\r\n                    device: str = \"auto\") -> pd.DataFrame:\r\n    \"\"\"Transcribe manifest rows, reusing every clip already in this model's cache.\"\"\"\r\n    TRANSCRIPTS.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\r\n    cache_path = TRANSCRIPTS / f\"{_slug(model_id)}__{quantisation}.parquet\"\r\n    done = pd.read_parquet(cache_path) if cache_path.exists() else pd.DataFrame(columns=[\"uid\"])\r\n    if \"asr_version\" in done:\r\n        done = done[done.asr_version == ASR_VERSION]\r\n    todo = rows[~rows.uid.isin(set(done.uid))]\r\n    if len(todo):\r\n        model = load_model(model_id, quantisation, device)\r\n        data = np.memmap(CACHE / \"audio_int16.bin\", dtype=np.int16, mode=\"r\")\r\n        out, started = [], time.time()\r\n        for n, row in enumerate(todo.itertuples(), 1):\r\n            audio = data[int(row.offset): int(row.offset) + int(row.n_samples)].astype(np.float32) / 32768.0\r\n            t0 = time.time()\r\n            result = transcribe_array(model, audio)\r\n            out.append({\"uid\": row.uid, **result, \"seconds\": time.time() - t0,\r\n                        \"audio_seconds\": row.n_samples / SR, \"model_id\": model_id,\r\n                        \"quantisation\": quantisation, \"asr_version\": ASR_VERSION})\r\n            if n % 100 == 0:\r\n                print(f\"  {model_id}: {n}/{len(todo)} clips, {time.time() - started:.0f} s\", flush=True)\r\n                pd.concat([done, pd.DataFrame(out)]).to_parquet(cache_path, index=False)\r\n        done = pd.concat([done, pd.DataFrame(out)], ignore_index=True)\r\n        done.to_parquet(cache_path, index=False)\r\n    return rows[[\"uid\"]].merge(done, on=\"uid\", how=\"left\")\r\n\r\n\r\ndef main():\r\n    p = argparse.ArgumentParser()\r\n    p.add_argument(\"--model\", required=True)\r\n    p.add_argument(\"--quantisation\", default=\"int8_float16\")\r\n    p.add_argument(\"--device\", default=\"auto\")\r\n    p.add_argument(\"--corpus\", default=\"atco2,atcosim,uwb\")\r\n    p.add_argument(\"--split\", default=\"test\")\r\n    args = p.parse_args()\r\n    manifest = pd.read_parquet(CACHE / \"manifest.parquet\")\r\n    rows = manifest[(manifest.split == args.split) & manifest.corpus.isin(args.corpus.split(\",\"))]\r\n    result = transcribe_rows(args.model, rows, args.quantisation, args.device)\r\n    print(f\"{len(result)} clips; real-time factor \"\r\n          f\"{result.seconds.sum() / max(result.audio_seconds.sum(), 1e-9):.3f}\")\r\n\r\n\r\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\r\n    main()\r\n"

      "path": "ml_pipeline/reports/voice_tuning_20261110/source_at_training/scripts/audit_voice_data.py",
      "sha256": "7627985e57a0087dc00c3943803cb85b2da54dcc2941c6a1857e399cee08a481",
      "bytes": 1994,
      "encoding": "utf-8",
      "content": "\"\"\"Audit exact decoded audio overlap; does not use test transcripts for tuning.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\nimport hashlib\nimport json\nfrom pathlib import Path\nimport sys\nimport numpy as np\n\nROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]\nsys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))\n\n\ndef main():\n    from ml_pipeline.voice.train_whisper import all_rows\n    from ml_pipeline.voice.build_audio_cache import CACHE, SR\n    rows = all_rows()\n    selected = rows[rows.split.isin([\"train\", \"val\", \"test\"])]\n    bins, seen, collisions = {}, {}, []\n    for row in selected.itertuples():\n        name = getattr(row, \"bin\", None)\n        name = name if isinstance(name, str) and name else \"audio_int16.bin\"\n        if name not in bins:\n            bins[name] = np.memmap(CACHE / name, dtype=np.int16, mode=\"r\")\n        start, length = int(row.offset), int(row.n_samples)\n        assert 0 <= start < start + length <= len(bins[name]), row.uid\n        assert 0 < length <= 30 * SR, row.uid\n        digest = hashlib.sha256(bins[name][start:start + length].tobytes()).hexdigest()\n        item = {\"uid\": row.uid, \"corpus\": row.corpus, \"split\": row.split}\n        previous = seen.setdefault(digest, item)\n        if previous[\"split\"] != row.split:\n            collisions.append({\"first\": previous, \"second\": item, \"sha256\": digest})\n    report = {\"clips_checked\": len(selected), \"distinct_exact_waveforms\": len(seen),\n              \"cross_split_exact_duplicates\": collisions,\n              \"limits\": \"Checks identical decoded PCM only, not near duplicates or shared unknown speakers. Test transcripts are not read for selection.\"}\n    out = ROOT / \"ml_pipeline/reports/voice_tuning_20261110/data_audit.json\"\n    out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    out.write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2), encoding=\"utf-8\")\n    print(json.dumps(report, indent=2))\n    if collisions:\n        raise SystemExit(\"Cross-split duplicates require review before reporting new results\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "ml_pipeline/reports/voice_tuning_20261110/source_at_training/scripts/experiment_voice_tuning.py",

      "bytes": 12996,
      "encoding": "utf-8",
      "content": "\"\"\"Local, validation-selected speech study. Never changes the application's model.\n\nRun with venv/Scripts/python.exe -u scripts/experiment_voice_tuning.py.\nTraining inputs remain read-only; new weights/caches live in checkpoints/voice_20261110.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport hashlib\nimport json\nimport os\nfrom pathlib import Path\nimport subprocess\nimport sys\nfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\n\nROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]\nsys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))\nWORK = ROOT / \"checkpoints/voice_20261110\"\nREPORT = ROOT / \"ml_pipeline/reports/voice_tuning_20261110\"\n\n\ndef save(path, value):\n    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    path.write_text(json.dumps(value, indent=2, default=str), encoding=\"utf-8\")\n\n\ndef command(args, log):\n    print(\"Running\", \" \".join(map(str, args)), flush=True)\n    with log.open(\"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as output:\n        subprocess.run([sys.executable, \"-u\", *map(str, args)], cwd=ROOT,\n                       stdout=output, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, check=True)\n\n\ndef sha(path):\n    return hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()\n\n\ndef freeze_selection(path, record):\n    if path.exists():\n        saved = json.loads(path.read_text())\n        for key in (\"winner\", \"model\", \"validation\"):\n            if saved[key] != record[key]:\n                raise RuntimeError(\"Frozen validation choice changed; use a new study directory\")\n        return saved\n    save(path, record)\n    return record\n\n\ndef choose(validation):\n    \"\"\"Predeclared: lower equal-corpus mean; at most 0.5 pp worse per real corpus.\"\"\"\n    baseline = validation[\"baseline\"]\n    eligible = [name for name, scores in validation.items()\n                if all(scores[c] <= baseline[c] + 0.5 for c in baseline)]\n    return min(eligible, key=lambda name: sum(validation[name].values()) / len(baseline))\n\n\ndef write_summary(validation, winner, baseline_test, selected_test):\n    lines = [\"# Speech recognition tuning \u2014 measured results\", \"\",\n             f\"Validation-selected result: **{winner}**. Lower word error is better.\", \"\",\n             \"## Comparison used to choose the model\", \"\",\n             \"All 1,632 real validation recordings, same application decoder for every model.\", \"\",\n             \"| Model | ATCOSIM word error % | UWB word error % |\",\n             \"| --- | ---: | ---: |\"]\n    for name, scores in validation.items():\n        lines.append(f\"| {name} | {scores['atcosim']} | {scores['uwb']} |\")\n    lines += [\"\", \"## Final test\", \"\", \"Only the validation winner received new test inference.\", \"\",\n              \"| Recording collection | Clips | Previous word error % | Selected word error % |\",\n              \"| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: |\"]\n    for corpus, scores in selected_test[\"corpora\"].items():\n        old = baseline_test[\"corpora\"][corpus]\n        lines.append(f\"| {corpus} | {scores['clips']} | {old['wer']} | {scores['wer']} |\")\n    old = baseline_test[\"corpora\"][\"atco2_clean\"]\n    new = selected_test[\"corpora\"][\"atco2_clean\"]\n    lines += [\"\", f\"Complete radio instructions correct: {old['clearance_accuracy']}% \u2192 {new['clearance_accuracy']}% \"\n              f\"on {new['clearance_n']} annotated instructions. This is a small sample.\", \"\",\n              \"Word error counts words substituted, omitted or added per 100 reference words; \"\n              \"it is not the percentage of whole sentences correct.\", \"\",\n              \"Indian everyday English is reported separately from radio speech. Generated Indian \"\n              \"practice voices cannot establish accuracy on real Indian controllers.\", \"\",\n              \"The historical test has been examined before; these results are exploratory. \"\n              \"Only two limited-budget training trials were searched, so no global-best claim is justified.\", \"\",\n              \"The application's installed speech model was not changed. New exported weights, \"\n              \"adapters and compressed runtime weights are in checkpoints/voice_20261110. \"\n              \"The UWB training source has noncommercial share-alike terms.\", \"\",\n              \"Recorded conflict detection remains 270/284 (95.1%), not remeasured.\"]\n    (REPORT / \"results.md\").write_text(\"\\n\".join(lines) + \"\\n\", encoding=\"utf-8\")\n\n\ndef main():\n    os.environ[\"HF_HUB_OFFLINE\"] = \"1\"\n    os.environ[\"TRANSFORMERS_OFFLINE\"] = \"1\"\n    import pandas as pd\n    from ml_pipeline.voice.train_whisper import all_rows, Audio\n    from ml_pipeline.voice.build_audio_cache import CACHE\n    from ml_pipeline.voice import transcribe as tr\n    from ml_pipeline.voice import evaluate_voice as ev\n    from ml_pipeline.voice.text_norm import normalise\n    WORK.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    REPORT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    baseline = CACHE / \"models/flightright-whisper-small-atc-p1\"\n    init = CACHE / \"runs/p1_main/best_adapter\"\n    rows = all_rows()\n    protocol = {\n        \"created_utc\": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),\n        \"seed\": 42, \"baseline\": str(baseline), \"initial_adapter\": str(init),\n        \"manifest_sha256\": sha(CACHE / \"manifest.parquet\"),\n        \"tts_manifest_sha256\": {p.name: sha(p) for p in sorted((CACHE / \"tts\").glob(\"manifest*.parquet\"))},\n        \"adapter_sha256\": sha(init / \"adapter_model.safetensors\"),\n        \"corpus_counts\": rows.groupby([\"corpus\", \"split\"]).size().to_dict().__str__(),\n        \"trials\": {\"continue_lr1e4\": \"p1\", \"indian_mix_lr5e5\": \"p2\"},\n        \"derived_candidates\": {\"uniform_soup3\": \"equal full-weight average of original, continue_lr1e4 and indian_mix_lr5e5\"},\n        \"training\": \"300 updates each, batch 8 x accumulation 2, warmup 30, evaluate every 100\",\n        \"selection\": \"lowest mean WER on ALL real ATCOSIM/UWB validation clips; no corpus > baseline+0.5 percentage points; baseline eligible\",\n        \"test_policy\": \"only validation winner gets new test inference; historical baseline cached; test never selects trial\",\n        \"decoder\": tr.DECODING, \"quantisation\": \"int8_float16\",\n        \"limits\": \"Historical test repeatedly inspected previously; not a fresh blind benchmark. Synthetic speech cannot validate real Indian ATC.\",\n        \"sources\": [\"https://huggingface.co/blog/fine-tune-whisper\",\n                    \"https://research.tudelft.nl/en/publications/whisper-atc-open-models-for-air-traffic-control-automatic-speech-/\",\n                    \"https://huggingface.co/datasets/Jzuluaga/uwb_atcc\"],\n        \"design\": \"Trial budget, learning rates and 0.5-point validation guard are our own experimental design, not a paper's guarantee.\",\n    }\n    if not (REPORT / \"protocol.json\").exists():\n        save(REPORT / \"protocol.json\", protocol)\n    else:\n        old = json.loads((REPORT / \"protocol.json\").read_text())\n        for key in (\"manifest_sha256\", \"adapter_sha256\", \"selection\", \"trials\"):\n            if old[key] != protocol[key]:\n                raise RuntimeError(f\"Study input/protocol changed: {key}\")\n        # Add provenance for still-unused synthetic data before its training trial begins.\n        if \"tts_manifest_sha256\" not in old:\n            assert not (WORK / \"indian_mix_lr5e5/state.json\").exists()\n            old[\"tts_manifest_sha256\"] = protocol[\"tts_manifest_sha256\"]\n            save(REPORT / \"protocol.json\", old)\n        elif old[\"tts_manifest_sha256\"] != protocol[\"tts_manifest_sha256\"]:\n            raise RuntimeError(\"Synthetic training manifest changed\")\n        if \"derived_candidates\" not in old:\n            assert not (REPORT / \"selection.json\").exists() and not (REPORT / \"test.json\").exists()\n            old[\"derived_candidates\"] = protocol[\"derived_candidates\"]\n            old[\"amendments\"] = [{\"utc\": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),\n                \"reason\": \"Full validation: real-only continuation 12.08/10.79 versus original 12.10/10.64; accent step-200 worsens recorded-speech validation while improving generated voices. Add one uniform weight average before any final test inference. Selection rule unchanged.\"}]\n            save(REPORT / \"protocol.json\", old)\n        elif old[\"derived_candidates\"] != protocol[\"derived_candidates\"]:\n            raise RuntimeError(\"Derived-candidate recipe changed\")\n    for corpus in (\"atcosim\", \"uwb\", \"tts_indian\"):\n        frame = rows[rows.corpus == corpus]\n        groups = {split: set(frame.loc[frame.split == split, \"group\"]) for split in (\"train\", \"val\", \"test\")}\n        for left, right in ((\"train\", \"val\"), (\"train\", \"test\"), (\"val\", \"test\")):\n            assert not groups[left] & groups[right], (corpus, left, right)\n    assert not rows.uid.duplicated().any(), \"Duplicate clip IDs\"\n    if \"--prepare-only\" in sys.argv:\n        print(\"Protocol frozen; split separation checked.\", flush=True)\n        return\n    audit_path = REPORT / \"data_audit.json\"\n    if not audit_path.exists():\n        command([\"scripts/audit_voice_data.py\"], REPORT / \"data_audit.log\")\n    assert not json.loads(audit_path.read_text())[\"cross_split_exact_duplicates\"], \"Audio leakage requires review\"\n    for name, stage in protocol[\"trials\"].items():\n        statefile = WORK / name / \"state.json\"\n        state = json.loads(statefile.read_text()) if statefile.exists() else {}\n        if not state.get(\"finished_utc\"):\n            args = [\"ml_pipeline/voice/train_whisper.py\", \"--stage\", stage, \"--init\", init,\n                    \"--output-root\", WORK, \"--run\", name, \"--lr\", \"0.0001\" if stage == \"p1\" else \"0.00005\",\n                    \"--warmup\", \"30\", \"--max-steps\", \"300\", \"--eval-every\", \"100\", \"--val-per-corpus\", \"200\"]\n            if statefile.exists():\n                args += [\"--resume\"]\n            command(args, REPORT / f\"{name}_train.log\")\n    models = {\"baseline\": str(baseline)}\n    for name in protocol[\"trials\"]:\n        out = WORK / \"models\" / name\n        if not (WORK / \"ct2\" / f\"local__{name}__int8_float16/model.bin\").exists():\n            command([\"ml_pipeline/voice/export_whisper.py\", \"--adapter\", WORK / name / \"best_adapter\",\n                     \"--name\", name, \"--output-root\", WORK / \"models\", \"--ct2-root\", WORK / \"ct2\"],\n                    REPORT / f\"{name}_export.log\")\n        models[name] = str(out)\n    soup = WORK / \"models/uniform_soup3\"\n    if not (WORK / \"ct2/local__uniform_soup3__int8_float16/model.bin\").exists():\n        command([\"ml_pipeline/voice/blend_whisper.py\", \"--sources\", *models.values(),\n                 \"--output\", soup, \"--ct2-root\", WORK / \"ct2\"], REPORT / \"uniform_soup3_export.log\")\n    models[\"uniform_soup3\"] = str(soup)\n    # Existing baseline converted model can be read; only new outputs use workspace paths.\n    original_ct2 = tr.CT2_DIR\n    original_transcripts = tr.TRANSCRIPTS\n    original_path = tr.ct2_path\n    tr.CT2_DIR = WORK / \"ct2\"\n    tr.TRANSCRIPTS = WORK / \"transcripts\"\n    tr.ct2_path = lambda model, quant: (original_ct2 / f\"{tr._slug(model)}__{quant}\"\n                                      if model == str(baseline) else original_path(model, quant))\n    val = rows[(rows.split == \"val\") & rows.corpus.isin([\"atcosim\", \"uwb\"])]\n    validation = {}\n    for name, model in models.items():\n        got = tr.transcribe_rows(model, val, device=\"cuda\").set_index(\"uid\")\n        scores = {c: ev.wer(list(f.text), [normalise(t) for t in got.loc[f.uid].hyp.fillna(\"\")])\n                  for c, f in val.groupby(\"corpus\")}\n        validation[name] = scores\n        print(\"VALIDATION\", name, scores, flush=True)\n        save(REPORT / \"validation.json\", validation)\n    winner = choose(validation)\n    runtime = (original_ct2 if winner == \"baseline\" else WORK / \"ct2\") / f\"{tr._slug(models[winner])}__int8_float16\"\n    freeze_selection(REPORT / \"selection.json\", {\"winner\": winner, \"model\": models[winner], \"runtime_model\": str(runtime), \"validation\": validation,\n                                                \"frozen_utc\": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()})\n    print(\"Frozen validation winner:\", winner, flush=True)\n    manifest = pd.read_parquet(CACHE / \"manifest.parquet\")\n    gold = ev.gold_atco2()\n    tr.TRANSCRIPTS = original_transcripts\n    baseline_test = ev.evaluate_model(str(baseline), manifest, gold, \"cuda\")\n    tr.TRANSCRIPTS = WORK / \"transcripts\"\n    winner_test = baseline_test if winner == \"baseline\" else ev.evaluate_model(models[winner], manifest, gold, \"cuda\")\n    save(REPORT / \"test.json\", {\"baseline\": baseline_test, \"selected\": winner_test, \"winner\": winner})\n    write_summary(validation, winner, baseline_test, winner_test)\n    save(REPORT / \"selected_artifact.json\", {\n        \"runtime_model\": str(runtime), \"configured_by\": \"FLIGHT_RIGHT_ASR_MODEL_ID\",\n        \"installed_in_application\": False,\n        \"files\": {p.name: {\"bytes\": p.stat().st_size, \"sha256\": sha(p)} for p in runtime.iterdir() if p.is_file()},\n        \"scope\": \"Offline research candidate; see validation and test results before installation\",\n        \"licence\": \"Includes UWB-ATCC CC BY-NC-SA 4.0 training data; noncommercial research use\",\n    })\n    print(\"Finished. Results:\", REPORT, flush=True)\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n"

      "bytes": 3365,
      "encoding": "utf-8",
      "content": "\"\"\"Paired recording/speaker bootstrap AFTER selection; never chooses a model.\"\"\"\nfrom pathlib import Path\nimport json\nimport sys\nimport jiwer\nimport numpy as np\nimport pandas as pd\n\nROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]\nsys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))\nfrom ml_pipeline.voice.build_audio_cache import CACHE\nfrom ml_pipeline.voice.evaluate_voice import gold_atco2\nfrom ml_pipeline.voice.text_norm import normalise\nfrom ml_pipeline.voice.transcribe import ASR_VERSION, _slug\n\n\ndef main():\n    report = ROOT / \"ml_pipeline/reports/voice_tuning_20261110\"\n    selection = json.loads((report / \"selection.json\").read_text())\n    assert (report / \"test.json\").exists(), \"Finish frozen-choice testing first\"\n    old_path = CACHE / \"transcripts/local__flightright-whisper-small-atc-p1__int8_float16.parquet\"\n    new_path = (old_path if selection[\"winner\"] == \"baseline\" else\n                ROOT / \"checkpoints/voice_20261110/transcripts\" / f\"{_slug(selection['model'])}__int8_float16.parquet\")\n    def read(path):\n        frame = pd.read_parquet(path)\n        return frame[frame.asr_version == ASR_VERSION].set_index(\"uid\")\n    old, new = read(old_path), read(new_path)\n    manifest = pd.read_parquet(CACHE / \"manifest.parquet\")\n    gold = gold_atco2()\n    cohorts = {\"atco2_clean\": gold[~gold.contaminated].assign(cluster=lambda f: f.recording)}\n    for corpus in (\"atcosim\", \"uwb\"):\n        cohorts[corpus] = manifest[(manifest.split == \"test\") & (manifest.corpus == corpus)].assign(cluster=lambda f: f.group)\n    output = {\"seed\": 42, \"resamples\": 2000, \"cohorts\": {},\n              \"interpretation\": \"Retrospective descriptive intervals on repeatedly inspected historical tests. ATCOSIM has only three held-out speakers. Ordinary Indian English omitted: no speaker IDs for a reliable speaker-cluster interval. These intervals do not guarantee future gains.\"}\n    for name, frame in cohorts.items():\n        groups = {}\n        for row in frame.itertuples():\n            if not row.text:\n                continue\n            errors = []\n            for predictions in (old, new):\n                comparison = jiwer.process_words(row.text, normalise(predictions.loc[row.uid, \"hyp\"]))\n                errors.append(comparison.substitutions + comparison.deletions + comparison.insertions)\n            group = groups.setdefault(row.cluster, np.zeros(3, dtype=np.float64))\n            group += [len(row.text.split()), *errors]\n        array = np.stack(list(groups.values()))\n        words, old_errors, new_errors = array.sum(axis=0)\n        rng = np.random.default_rng(42)\n        draws = array[rng.integers(0, len(array), size=(2000, len(array)))].sum(axis=1)\n        differences = 100 * (draws[:, 2] - draws[:, 1]) / draws[:, 0]\n        output[\"cohorts\"][name] = {\"clips\": len(frame), \"clusters\": len(array),\n            \"reference_words\": int(words), \"previous_errors\": int(old_errors), \"selected_errors\": int(new_errors),\n            \"word_error_change_percentage_points\": round(float(100 * (new_errors - old_errors) / words), 4),\n            \"paired_cluster_95_percent_interval\": np.percentile(differences, [2.5, 97.5]).round(4).tolist(),\n            \"negative_change_means_fewer_errors\": True}\n    (report / \"uncertainty.json\").write_text(json.dumps(output, indent=2), encoding=\"utf-8\")\n    print(json.dumps(output, indent=2))\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "ml_pipeline/reports/voice_tuning_20261110/source_snapshot.json",
      "sha256": "43c2c84000a07d3b3293a6aa223b7f2387ac49551ac0ca565a2e314d486c9bf1",
      "bytes": 1209,
      "encoding": "utf-8",
      "content": "{\r\n  \"git_base\": \"73be168a2457121e9fdfe0dbe61b81ccd903abdd\",\r\n  \"finalized_utc\": \"2026-11-10T11:15:45.812308+00:00\",\r\n  \"sources_sha256\": {\r\n    \"ml_pipeline/voice/train_whisper.py\": \"62df9d14c7b8077961d45e30cc22683ee5c28d3c53d537b5a6d2dbbfe81c725d\",\r\n    \"ml_pipeline/voice/export_whisper.py\": \"9c24b13fbd55bb67f0ae7b953ee8c69ce79b37183c3ed32c99a46f0b33a7bcc6\",\r\n    \"ml_pipeline/voice/blend_whisper.py\": \"c293025fbfd4bc673d745be33cb37eddfc4e7f11b1e7372148fa616cd4a4dc04\",\r\n    \"ml_pipeline/voice/transcribe.py\": \"26a48b4a6425968325671d5089c0d2f45bbea4873d2e3c7fe41e4f18e889967e\",\r\n    \"scripts/experiment_voice_tuning.py\": \"d502dbf38252bed4428edaf280d626a26a7c41e8fa04ea669f68bc5b0134bcf2\",\r\n    \"scripts/audit_voice_data.py\": \"7627985e57a0087dc00c3943803cb85b2da54dcc2941c6a1857e399cee08a481\",\r\n    \"scripts/report_voice_uncertainty.py\": \"02a05cd12833d7caa73273752243d61ee0c6129d95b56455aee3e44d25cd8af2\",\r\n    \"ml_pipeline/tests/test_voice_training.py\": \"4a3758d5f8dc0f2b996889462c77ff87e05dd156d697b776f44ac5788aa02883\"\r\n  },\r\n  \"unit_test_log_sha256\": \"f0ef74d4730a236b8af51cfa837efcef4d9cef6bd785dd3ea3fd088dd80539cf\",\r\n  \"note\": \"Final reproduction source; all changes remain local and uncommitted\"\r\n}"

      "current_summary": "Served default: uniform_soup3, user-authorized 11 Nov; equal full-weight average of original P1 and two independent 300-update continuations, selected on 1,632 validation clips before test. 10 Nov tests: ATCO2 clean WER 27.95%, ATCOSIM 4.80%, UWB 15.33% (regression from 15.11%), Svarah 31.46%; complete instructions 21/31 versus 20/31. Generated practice WER 24.62% to 16.60% on 416 development calls, not real Indian radio accuracy. Original test scores are cached re-scoring, with 256 fresh controls matching exactly. Small/mixed retrospective effects; no new latency claim.",
      "narrative_markdown": "## November 2026 continuation: current served model and complete experiment\n\n**Current default: uniform_soup3, activated on 11 November at the user's explicit request.** This is the validation-selected combined model. Independent test results are mixed; activation does not turn the UWB regression into an improvement. Original P1 weights remain available as fallback. The prediction target, text normalizer, decoder and alert thresholds are unchanged.\n\nThe purpose was to improve speech-to-text using existing recorded speech and previously unused Indian-accent generated practice calls. Two independent continuations start from the original best P1 adapter, not from each other. A third candidate averages the full weights of the original and both continuations equally. Identical tensors stay bit-exact; tied weights remain tied. All three source weight hashes were verified unchanged before/after averaging (287 tensors preserved, 192 averaged). The average is one ordinary Whisper model, not an inference ensemble.\n\n### How the data and comparisons were kept fair\n\nBase: openai/whisper-small; original adapter: local voice cache runs/p1_main/best_adapter. Exact base/adapter identities, original manifest hashes and synthetic-manifest hashes are in the protocol evidence. The exact pre-change training/source files were preserved under the archived source_at_training paths; the source snapshot pins their SHA-256 values.\n\n| Corpus | Split | Clips | Hours |\n| --- | --- | --- | --- |\n| atco2 | test | 871 | 0.987 |\n| atcosim | test | 1712 | 2.045 |\n| atcosim | train | 7330 | 7.765 |\n| atcosim | val | 516 | 0.636 |\n| svarah | test | 6656 | 9.614 |\n| tts_indian | train | 3341 | 4.933 |\n| tts_indian | val | 416 | 0.599 |\n| uwb | test | 2814 | 2.428 |\n| uwb | train | 10172 | 9.485 |\n| uwb | val | 1116 | 1.048 |\n\nATCOSIM is human speech in simulation: train sm1/sm2/sm4/zf1/zf2, validation gm1/gf1, test sm3/zf3/gm2. UWB keeps published recording-disjoint test data, drops the overlapping recording, and chooses validation recordings by the existing SHA-1 modulo-10 rule. Speaker identities are unavailable for UWB. No ATCO2 audio is used for training; 559 mirror clips remain excluded. ATCO2 test has 762 primary clean clips and 109 clips contaminated for another published comparator, not for this model. Both subsets remain reported. Svarah is test-only ordinary Indian English, not aviation radio; accent and vocabulary are confounded.\n\nGenerated practice validation has 416 calls, separate aircraft identities and three held-out generated voices (Ravi, Riya, Vikas). Noise comes only from UWB training audio. This is not real Indian controller speech. Training has 3,341 generated calls. The audio audit checked 34,944 usable decoded waveforms: all distinct, no exact cross-split duplicates, valid bounds and durations. It does not exclude all possible near-duplicates or shared unknown speakers. All 22,891 training/validation targets fit: maximum 49 tokens, zero over the trainer limit.\n\n### Every training trial and checkpoint\n\nBoth use seed 42, LoRA rank 32/alpha 64/dropout .05 on q/k/v/out and fc1/fc2 projections (12,976,128 trainable parameters), FP16 autocast, gradient checkpointing, AdamW weight decay .01, gradient clipping 1.0, batch 8 accumulated twice (effective batch 16), warmup 30, at most 300 optimizer updates, evaluation every 100 and patience 3. A new optimizer/schedule is used for each continuation; this is initialization from P1, not a resumed legacy optimizer. The starting checkpoint stays eligible. Checkpoint selection uses the mean of the two recorded-speech validation WERs; generated speech never chooses a checkpoint. Training monitoring uses a fixed seed-42 sample of 200 clips per corpus and greedy GPU decoding; these numbers are distinct from the full production-decoder validation/test scores.\n\n| Trial | Stage | LR | Updates completed | Best update | Available train pool | Weighted epoch pool |\n| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |\n| continue_lr1e4 | p1 | 0.0001 | 300 | 300 | {\"atcosim\": 7330, \"uwb\": 10172} | {\"atcosim\": 7330, \"uwb\": 10172} |\n| indian_mix_lr5e5 | p2 | 5e-05 | 300 | 100 | {\"atcosim\": 7330, \"tts_indian\": 3341, \"uwb\": 10172} | {\"atcosim\": 1832, \"tts_indian\": 3341, \"uwb\": 2543} |\n\nP1 uses all recorded ATCOSIM/UWB data in the epoch pool. P2 uses all generated data and 25% of each recorded corpus per epoch, then stops after the stated 300 updates. Available pool size is not a claim that every item was consumed in this short continuation. P2 update 100 was retained: later generated-speech gains worsened recorded-speech validation.\n\n| Trial | Update | ATCOSIM WER % | UWB WER % | Generated WER % | Real-corpus mean % | Measured UTC |\n| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |\n| continue_lr1e4 | 0 | 12.98 | 12.12 | not applicable | 12.55 | 2026-11-10T08:18:11.768958+00:00 |\n| continue_lr1e4 | 100 | 13.06 | 11.77 | not applicable | 12.41 | 2026-11-10T08:29:12.722182+00:00 |\n| continue_lr1e4 | 200 | 12.67 | 12.07 | not applicable | 12.37 | 2026-11-10T08:40:09.467943+00:00 |\n| continue_lr1e4 | 300 | 12.4 | 12.27 | not applicable | 12.34 | 2026-11-10T08:50:57.708954+00:00 |\n| indian_mix_lr5e5 | 0 | 12.98 | 12.12 | 25.72 | 12.55 | 2026-11-10T08:55:31.793245+00:00 |\n| indian_mix_lr5e5 | 100 | 12.75 | 12.32 | 5.39 | 12.54 | 2026-11-10T09:10:06.431531+00:00 |\n| indian_mix_lr5e5 | 200 | 13.18 | 12.52 | 3.67 | 12.85 | 2026-11-10T09:25:13.705290+00:00 |\n| indian_mix_lr5e5 | 300 | 13.68 | 12.17 | 3.49 | 12.93 | 2026-11-10T09:37:40.092494+00:00 |\n\n### Validation selection, before final-test inference\n\nExport each candidate to CTranslate2 int8_float16. On all 1,632 recorded validation clips (516 ATCOSIM and 1,116 UWB), use identical English transcribe, beam 5, temperature 0, no timestamps, no prior-text conditioning and no VAD (`fw1-beam5-v1`). Choose the lowest equal-corpus mean WER, allowing at most 0.5 percentage points regression per real corpus; the original stays eligible. Generated voices and final-test scores do not select the winner.\n\n| Configuration | ATCOSIM validation WER % | UWB validation WER % | Outcome |\n| --- | --- | --- | --- |\n| baseline | 12.1 | 10.64 | unselected |\n| continue_lr1e4 | 12.08 | 10.79 | unselected |\n| indian_mix_lr5e5 | 12.67 | 10.99 | unselected |\n| uniform_soup3 | 11.92 | 10.41 | selected |\n\nThe uniform average was a documented validation-only amendment after the real-only continuation failed to beat the original on full validation and P2 showed forgetting. The selection rule stayed fixed. The coordinator restarted with 100 P2 validation clips cached; training and exports were already complete, no progress was lost, and no selection/test report existed. Final choice frozen at 2026-11-10T10:08:20.785274+00:00. Only that winner received new full test inference.\n\n### Final test: measurements from 10 November, activation on 11 November\n\nWER counts substitutions, deletions and insertions per 100 reference words; lower is better, and it is not sentence accuracy. Do not combine corpora into one accuracy number. The selected model freshly transcribed 12,053 test clips. Original-model predictions were cached and re-scored using the same current normalizer/extractor, not newly inferred in full. Fresh original-model controls on 64 clips in each of four corpora (256 total, seed 42) matched cached normalized text and log-probability exactly. Timing includes cached and concurrent-load observations; it is not a new isolated latency benchmark.\n\n| Corpus | Clips | Original WER % | Current WER % | Original numeral-normalized % | Current numeral-normalized % |\n| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |\n| atco2_clean | 762 | 28.29 | 27.95 | 28.29 | 27.95 |\n| atco2_contaminated | 109 | 17.4 | 17.34 | 17.4 | 17.34 |\n| atcosim_heldout_speakers | 1712 | 4.88 | 4.8 | 4.88 | 4.8 |\n| uwb_heldout_recordings | 2814 | 15.11 | 15.33 | 15.11 | 15.33 |\n| svarah_indian_english | 6656 | 33.17 | 31.46 | 28.9 | 27.22 |\n\nComplete callsign + action + value instructions on ATCO2 clean: **20/31 (64.52%) -> 21/31 (67.74%)**. Plausible-tag subset: 19/24 -> 20/24. The small, imperfectly tagged instruction subset is not a certification or a measured end-to-end runtime alert gain. Callsign matching uses the same proxy list from annotated airport/frequency/day for both models, not a prompt to the recognizer. All action, value, callsign, extraction-agreement and per-first-language results remain verbatim in the test evidence. No accuracy is invented for unselected models' tests: not run.\n\nGenerated-voice development diagnostic, after selection froze: 416 held-out generated calls, same runtime decoder, original WER **24.62%**, selected **16.60%**. This does not measure real Indian controllers. No real Indian radio benchmark was available/used in this study.\n\n### Uncertainty, rejected work and implementation checks\n\n| Corpus | Clusters | Word-error change, percentage points | Paired cluster 95% interval |\n| --- | --- | --- | --- |\n| atco2_clean | 452 | -0.3406 | [-0.825, 0.1593] |\n| atcosim | 3 | -0.0884 | [-0.1348, -0.0708] |\n| uwb | 676 | 0.2171 | [-0.0797, 0.4924] |\n\nPaired cluster percentile bootstrap: 2,000 resamples, seed 42, recording clusters for ATCO2/UWB and speaker clusters for ATCOSIM. Negative changes mean fewer errors. ATCOSIM has only three test speakers. No speaker-level Svarah interval is claimed because IDs are absent. The ATCO2/UWB intervals include zero; these small effects are uncertain. The historical test has been inspected in earlier work, so this is retrospective exploratory evidence, not a fresh blind benchmark.\n\nUnselected/rejected work is retained: real-only continuation; accent continuation and its later worse checkpoints; four-worker speed probe (290 matching transcripts/confidences but no consistent speed gain); initial batch probe with a feature-cropping error; corrected batch probe with 14/128, 13/128 and 14/128 transcript differences. Neither speed variant was adopted. The batch findings describe our experimental code, not a library defect. Official scores always use the unchanged sequential production decoder.\n\nTrainer repairs: final partial gradient groups are processed/scaled, exact data cursor and random/scaler state are saved, legacy non-exact resumes are refused, loading failures propagate, initial weights stay eligible, synthetic WER does not select real-radio checkpoints, and existing runs/exports are not silently overwritten. Export supports isolated output paths; runtime loading accepts an already converted complete folder. All 90 pre-activation Python tests passed, including nine focused training/selection/averaging checks. Today's activation check loaded the actual configured SpeechService and reproduced evaluated ATCOSIM/UWB transcripts.\n\nHardware/software: {\"gpu\": \"NVIDIA GeForce RTX 4050 Laptop GPU\", \"gpu_memory_bytes\": 6438780928, \"packages\": {\"accelerate\": \"1.15.0\", \"ctranslate2\": \"4.8.2\", \"faster-whisper\": \"1.2.1\", \"jiwer\": \"4.0.0\", \"numpy\": \"2.5.2\", \"pandas\": \"3.0.5\", \"peft\": \"0.21.0\", \"torch\": \"2.13.0+cu126\", \"transformers\": \"5.17.0\"}, \"python\": \"3.13.2 (tags/v3.13.2:4f8bb39, Feb  4 2025, 15:23:48) [MSC v.1942 64 bit (AMD64)]\", \"torch_cuda\": \"12.6\"}.\n\n### Current deployment, artifact identity and reproduction\n\nRuntime folder: `C:\\Users\\singh\\OneDrive\\Desktop\\Archit\\KJ Somaiya\\LY Project\\Flight_Right\\checkpoints\\voice_20261110\\ct2\\local__uniform_soup3__int8_float16`. ## Evaluation update 2026-11-11

This dated addition supplements the unchanged earlier history. Latest deployment statements below supersede earlier deployment status only.

## Activation and append-only verification: 11 November 2026

All 95 Python tests passed. Earlier comparison and speech-ledger prefixes remain byte-for-byte intact; refresh is idempotent. The GPU speech service loaded uniform_soup3 and reproduced two saved validation transcripts (see activation_runtime_check.json evidence). No persistent backend process was found or restarted; the default applies when the backend starts. These are software/integration checks, not a new accuracy or latency benchmark. The preceding update heading uses UTC 10 November, corresponding to local 11 November.

The earlier link to asr_small_atc_20261110.md is historical: the complete continuation study now appears in this existing ledger. Standalone report cleanup is separately user-authorized; weights, original datasets and prediction caches are retained. Test log is preserved verbatim in the evidence capsule.

## Evaluation update 2026-11-11

This dated addition supplements the unchanged earlier history. Latest deployment statements below supersede earlier deployment status only.

## Standalone report cleanup verified: 11 November 2026

At the user's separate request, removed 63 checksum-verified standalone study/report/source-cache files (481,455 bytes) after preserving their exact contents in this existing ledger. All cleanup paths are absent, and the working study report directory is gone. No weights, original datasets or runtime prediction caches were removed. The study command now exits with the archived selection without restarting training or recreating reports; the uncertainty command successfully reads its archived results and explicitly labels them historical. Both commands returned exit code 0. All 288 unique evidence files and all 11 pinned model artifacts verified after cleanup. The 95-test suite passed before cleanup; it was not rerun after deleting report-only files. No new accuracy inference was performed in this cleanup step.

## Evaluation update 2026-11-11

This dated addition supplements the unchanged earlier history. Latest deployment statements below supersede earlier deployment status only.

## Reporting-rule alignment: 11 November 2026

The revised AGENTS.md supersedes the earlier blanket append-only rule for docs/evaluation.md. That file now contains one current comparison: selected configuration results, measurement dates, limitations and served/candidate status. Superseded speech checkpoints and duplicate comparison updates were removed from the summary; their full experiments and original evidence remain in the individual histories. Superseded served LSTM and multi-path score rows also remain in their existing model histories, with selected candidate scores shown in the summary and undeployed status explicit. The publisher replaces the current summary and is idempotent. Individual ledger writing continues to preserve all experiment entries and raw evidence, rejecting silent historical changes. The new same-experiment improvement exception was not exercised: no improved rerun was performed or claimed.

Five affected publisher/deployment/archive tests passed, including obsolete-summary removal, one selected speech checkpoint across corpora, idempotence, unchanged history prefixes and rejection of changed history. Every existing model ledger was byte-for-byte unchanged before adding this verification entry; all 288 pre-existing evidence records remain unchanged. No retraining, transcription, score remeasurement, model/default change or file cleanup was performed. Previous 95-test run remains historical; only the five affected tests were rerun for this reporting change.

## Pretrained demo portability check - 11 November 2026

Experiment `pretrained_demo_portability_20261111` checks packaging of the existing
served artifacts; it does not retrain, select a new model or remeasure accuracy.
Baseline: served configuration from source checkpoint `67a236d`, with the same
weight bytes, features, calibration, decoder and alert thresholds. Exact model
SHA-256 values and paths are in `demo/manifest.json`; code hashes, installed
versions, unsuccessful attempts and observed outcomes are in `demo/verification.json`.

Input: the separately licensed adsb.lol replay described by `demo/provenance.json`,
5,548 measured/bucketed reports from 98 aircraft, 4 September 2026 20:45-22:15 UTC.
This is a demonstration input, not a train/validation/test split or a new accuracy
benchmark. No new seeds or hyperparameters: not applicable. One synthetic practice
WAV was passed through the actual selected speech runtime on CPU, without a cached
transcript or an applied instruction. Real radio accuracy and latency: not measured.

Acceptance passed: fresh Python 3.13 CPU environment installed from the launcher,
pip check, frontend dependency install and HTTP response, all 131 asset hashes in
a clean checkout without .env/private data, all three core trained artifacts loaded,
speech ready on CPU, a selected trajectory returned ready, and replay pair assessment
ran. The residual LSTM was loaded, not separately scored. All 115 Python tests and
the frontend build passed. SQLite is used; database-enforced append-only protection
is not claimed. This smoke check does not supersede any measured result.

Limitations: the shareable replay differs from the restricted OpenSky recording;
no receiver/browser audio capture or macOS/Linux verification. npm ci reported
20 existing dependency advisories, including one critical; unrelated forced
upgrades were not performed. Commit/push is awaiting the user's explicit instruction.

Original evidence `demo/verification.json`, SHA-256 `ceaa9c986f60695746523d095f268717280caa5aeaed384037731868f03280f9`.

## Repository delivery cleanup — 11 November 2026

Packaging check, not a new model benchmark. Consolidated 108 route records into one bundled cache; all original records and destination-feature arrays match. All 24 asset hashes passed. The same served artifacts loaded; no weights, preprocessing formulas, thresholds, model selection or measured headline changed. 119 Python and seven radio tests passed; frontend build and npm ci passed; npm audit reports zero vulnerabilities. Eleven existing React lint errors and eight warnings remain; browser visual verification was unavailable. Seeds, training hyperparameters and new accuracy/latency measurements: not applicable/not run. Exact evidence follows. Original experiment entries and raw evidence are preserved.
